within us? All things then must necessarily be in a peaceable and holy order, and blessed subjection. Happy are those whose inward eyes are opened to see, possess, and enjoy all of this, even when withdrawn from all human society <note>original: "Creaturely Society"</note>, and so transported into the company of the seraphic spirits <note>Seraphim are traditionally the highest rank of angels, associated with light, ardor, and purity.</note> in heavenly places.

<margin>Seven days of solitude, avoiding all human society as much as possible.</margin>
Since I have set myself apart to be alone, a full gathering now continually meets me—such company as makes me spend my hours and days with all spiritual mirth and joy. For I have now truly come (without pretense <note>original: "Flattering"</note> or temporary sparks that quickly go out) unto the everlasting, burning throne of the Godhead. There, just and perfect spirits exist like so many lesser flames, appearing in great numbers as God’s encompassing host, ministering everything that may delight me. So I find I am to be reserved for communion at God’s throne <note>original: "Throne-Conversation"</note>, which is like the seven-day Feast of Tabernacles <note>A biblical festival (Sukkot) celebrating God’s dwelling with His people; it was a time of rest and celebration.</note>, during which no ordinary labor <note>original: "servile work"</note> is to be thought upon. This is a time for a holy assembly and for celebrating the praises of the Lamb, who alone has achieved the victory for us.

*Oh! What shall hinder <note>original: "let"</note> me now from sounding the trumpet and giving a great shout within myself, that the Lord Almighty has come to reign? Rejoice, you heavens—without, within, and all around—for the Lord Jehovah alone has done it! He has made wars to cease and keeps the enemy outside the gates. Oh mighty Prince and Savior! You have entered into your throne-rest! You have truly become Lord of the Sabbath, and therefore you have the power to make it eternal, as a memorial of your redeeming blood, which has become the life-giving <note>original: "quickning"</note> and reigning life. Maintain my love forever in this new governing sphere with you; for I never knew such a pleasant state until the time you brought your kingdom into me, to rule over everything in peace and love, and to keep every spirit in check and in awe. Your throne is set, your council is established, the Urim and Thummim <note>Ancient objects used by Israelite priests to determine God’s will; here they symbolize perfect divine judgment.</note> of sound and right judgment has come down, and decrees have gone forth, which all-powerful might will ensure are executed within this hidden kingdom of my soul and mind. The vision of all this which has now arrived, the Spirit in Christ—my Lord’s own personality—gave me to see, and therefore to prophesy, hoping the time is at hand; and it is so in good earnest. For I am made to understand that the Holy and Just One has come, in his first and early reign, to put all things right that were found*

<summary>The author describes a period of spiritual solitude that leads to a profound visionary experience of God's throne and internal kingdom. She celebrates the "Feast of Tabernacles" within her soul, declaring that Christ has taken His seat as the internal ruler, bringing peace, order, and divine judgment.</summary>
<keywords>mysticism, solitude, Seraphim, Feast of Tabernacles, internal kingdom, Urim and Thummim, spiritual warfare, divine rest</keywords>

--- Page 2 ---
<meta>This page continues the author’s mystical reflection on the transition from a state of internal conflict and "fallen nature" to a state of divine harmony and spiritual governance within the soul.</meta>

...in confusion, within the fallen characteristics of nature, and to harmonize whatever emerged from the bitter root of one's essence in discord and strife. Oh! To have a cure arrive to relieve this fallen, weary, exhausted life—which was once in a state of confused conflict—can only cause triumph wherever such a dominion is recognized and entered into, even if it is invisible. No one is aware of what happens or is performed there except for the obedient subjects who wait upon the throne and are ready to fulfill all the royal laws expressed by the counsel of the Trinity <note>The Christian doctrine of the three-in-one God: Father, Son, and Holy Spirit.</note>. 

And truly, I find great joy in adhering to these restrictions and ordinances that belong to the Lord Christ’s kingdom. These are quite different from—and of another kind than—what was previously allowed and overlooked <note>original: "winked at"</note> by the Just One. This occurred before the Word (which is *Spirit and Life* <note>A reference to John 6:63: "The words that I speak unto you, they are spirit, and they are life."</note>) declared to me that he had come to make a total change and to discard all the religious forms and systems <note>original: "Forms and Constitutions"</note> that have passed for heavenly structures. 

Yet these old forms never reached this new, most perfect, and excellent kingdom. This kingdom is so crystal-clear <note>original: "Chrystaline"</note> that the spirit and soul are realized within an encompassing <note>original: "circuled"</note> glory. They are so enclosed that they no longer look out or descend from this high-mounted white throne, upon which the reins of a great and wonderful government are to be held fast. I have received many words of caution regarding this, for it is "abiding"—remaining steady in this state—that will produce the most glorious victory. 

For no one—even those who have grown to the highest maturity <note>original: "Stature"</note> in Christ and are ready to govern—is yet exempt from the assaults and temptations that come from the satanic, earthly kingdom. In that realm, all carnal, earthly senses and intellectual cunning <note>original: "rational Subtilty"</note> still lie in wait. If the exalted spirit sitting upon the throne with Christ should venture down and degrade itself by complying with the worldly laws and principles <note>original: "Rudiments"</note> it has already rejected, it is possible for the kingdom to be torn away. This happened in the case of King Saul <note>The first king of Israel, who lost his kingdom after disobeying God's commands.</note>. Even with Solomon himself, God would not overlook it when he turned away from the Lord in any degree; instead, he received a stern rebuke for it. 

All of this was laid before me so that I would hold fast to the scepter <note>A staff held by a monarch as a symbol of sovereignty.</note> in humility, purity, fear, righteousness, and holy courage. My goal is to endure in this first and invisible reign of the Lamb of God <note>A title for Jesus Christ, emphasizing his sacrifice and eventual triumph.</note>, so that he may eventually be visibly magnified, for

<summary>This page explores the internal transformation required to live within Christ’s mystical kingdom, emphasizing the need for constant spiritual vigilance. The author warns that even highly advanced spirits can lose their divine standing if they succumb to worldly temptations, citing the biblical failures of Saul and Solomon as cautionary tales.</summary>
<keywords>Mysticism, Internal Kingdom, Spiritual Discipline, Trinity, King Saul, King Solomon, Temptation, Christ's Reign, Sovereignty</keywords>

--- Page 3 ---
<meta>This page continues the author’s explanation of the internal kingdom of Christ, contrasting its spiritual authority with the worldly influences of the "starry government" or astrological fate.</meta>

<page-num>(73)</page-num>

...the conviction of those who are still alienated by a spirit of unbelief, not knowing the peaceful fruits of this internal kingdom. This kingdom can in no way mix with what belongs to the *starry government* <note>original: "Starry Government"; in 17th-century mysticism, this refers to the influence of the stars and planets over the physical world and the fallen human nature.</note>, as it is of a completely different spirit. It neither cares for nor seeks *ruling power* or *status* from these lower planets, which grant the *world's wealth* and *honor* to the most wicked and lowly of men. Therefore, it is little regarded by those who only have eyes to see the workings of the superior *constellations* that move in the third heaven, far from all mortal sight. From that heaven alone I have sought honor, credit, and reputation. And now I am greatly satisfied to be considered of no reputation, and to be like a poor *Lazarus* <note>A reference to the beggar in Jesus's parable (Luke 16), who was poor on earth but comforted in heaven.</note> for the joy of that kingdom, which I already possess. 

Yet I do not stop here, as if I did not expect and believe that this invisible kingdom will eventually manifest so visibly that it may make the *sun, moon*, and *stars* of this lower *world* bow before it. To be assured of this, we can desire no fuller nor better evidence than the total and victorious reign of God over all our sin-infected qualities. For once this is finished, it will certainly introduce the visible reign of the Holy *Spirit*, who will not fear to face the "locusts" of the earth who have received their abundance and blessings from the *beast's kingdom* <note>A reference to the worldly and corrupt power structures described in the Book of Revelation.</note>, whose glory will certainly wither and die away. 

Indeed, those who are among the *Israelites* <note>Here meaning the community of believers.</note> and yet still live in the abomination of *self-love* will be so ashamed and confused at the opening of this plentiful treasury. It will shower down so abundantly upon those who sit on the throne with *Christ* that all those who have hoarded these worldly goods will feel such indignation against themselves that they shall throw their gold and silver to the "bats and moles" of the earth <note>A reference to Isaiah 2:20, meaning they will discard their idols and earthly wealth in shame.</note>. They will take hold of the robes of those ruling from the throne and cry out to share in their lot and portion. For this spiritual substance will be of such quality and will increase daily, satisfying every need of the physical body. Thus, *Jacob’s* "all and enough" <note>A reference to Genesis 33:11, signifying a state of complete divine blessing and sufficiency.</note> will most surely be possessed without the curse or sorrow that has always accompanied the visible wealth gathered by the worldly wisdom and skill of man. People have so idolized the works of their own hands that they have completely lost those blessings which come by surrendering their entire mind and will to the Lord’s dominion.

<summary>The author argues that the internal spiritual kingdom is superior to worldly status and wealth, predicting a future where this invisible reality becomes visible, causing the worldly to abandon their riches in favor of divine grace.</summary>

--- Page 4 ---
<meta>This page continues the author’s discussion of the internal spiritual kingdom, moving from the rejection of worldly status to the promise of divine transformation and future visible glory.</meta>

<page-num>74</page-num>

This is the enduring kingdom that shall spread from the root of *Christ*, rising up in his perfect stature within and throughout the soul's essence to deify <note>original: "deifie"; to make divine or god-like through union with God.</note> it. This is done so that the soul may remain in the unalterable substance of a divine nature <note>original: "Godded Nature"</note>, without the risk of ever falling back into sin, the curse, or sorrowful weakness. This will be the most wonderful salvation that has ever yet been revealed. For this reason, it will necessarily follow that this visible kingdom will also be established and become like a raised banner <note>original: "Ensign"</note>, so that all mortal flesh—which once acted so insultingly and arrogantly—may tremble and fall before it. 

Where the *Lord* had only come in his first reign, no show of glory was visible to outward appearance; instead, there were dark clouds of suffering. These sufferings, when used rightly, make us fit <note>original: "meet"</note> for the full possession of the first and second kingdoms, each in its high degree <note>original: "graduation"</note>, leading toward glorification. This is how things are now in the hidden spheres <note>original: "Sphears"</note>, where the high glories of the throne are manifested in a kingly pomp and splendor quite different from anything that has ever been, or could possibly be, imagined here, until the *Kingdom* itself works its way out visibly. Then we shall know why *God* knows us as he does now, in preparation for this excellent reign, which is to match the royalty, purity, and pomp that *God* himself now possesses among his glorified *saints* and *angels*. 

I am fully assured that *God* has chosen <note>original: "taken into his Eye and Love election"</note> at this very day those who shall come to sit in princely majesty and great *dominion* within the internal *government* <note>original: "Regency"</note> of the *soul*, conquering the resisting motions of sin and their own passions. For maintaining *faithfulness, truth*, and *love* here will be sufficient signs and evidence of what *God* will further exalt us to. In the meantime, whoever has attained the first *kingdom*—in *wisdom, sanctification*, and excellent *charity* <note>original: "Charity"; here meaning divine love or "agape."</note>—should know that these virtues will provide the powerful momentum <note>original: "onset"</note> for entering into the furthest reaches of this everlasting *kingdom*. We should not think it enough merely to pray for this; rather, we should do all that is required for it in proper order and with fixed resolution. We must hold fast to what we have already gained, so that no one may rob us <note>original: "supplant"</note> of that which will be the raised *crown*, visible everywhere upon the heads of the anointed ones.

<summary>This page describes the growth of Christ’s kingdom within the soul, explaining how internal victory over sin prepares the believer for a future state of divine glory and "deification." The author emphasizes that current faithfulness in the face of suffering is the evidence of one's future exaltation to a princely, divine nature.</summary>

### *Here follow divine revelations <note>original: "Openings"</note> regarding the reign and kingdom of love.*

I have been engaged for a considerable time in the prophetic office and ministry <note>original: "Ministration"</note>, guided by the spirit of revelation, as my writings testify. I have written concerning the Kingdom of Christ, first in its state of suffering, and then in its rise through tribulation toward victory, dominion, and glorification through Love. The stages <note>original: "degrees"</note> leading to this have been fully described by the author in *The Heavenly Cloud* (printed in 1680). That work has had powerful effects in bringing about and establishing this kingdom. In that kingdom, the spirit of prophecy was given as a confirming <note>original: "sealing"</note> evidence that the Spirit of Truth was the source of those previous revelations. 

These revelations are now being confirmed by the present reign of a risen Christ within certain individuals who are and shall be chosen for this purpose: to demonstrate the coming of that most excellent and perfect state, which is the Dominion of Love. This dominion has exalted that same prophetic spirit—now seated with Christ upon the throne—and rules absolutely through God’s gentle love over the entire realm, even to the furthest reaches of the soul’s inner world <note>original: "Earth"</note>. There, great peace is now found, as the government is carried out serenely and calmly through conquering love, which has overflowed the whole earth like a river. But this was not so fully felt, known, and witnessed until recently. 

The previous reign was maintained in an imperfect state. The internal spirits <note>the various faculties and drives of the human soul</note> were kept in check by awe and were unwillingly made subject to the kingdom's laws, which were renewed to suit this most eminent and unique ministry. While I was under that reign—which was viewed as sharp and severe—my whole being was held on such a tight <note>original: "straight"</note> rein that it tended toward resistance <note>original: "reluctancy"</note>. This would have broken all harmony among the spiritual qualities <note>original: "properties"</note> that should exist in a newly modeled soul. In this way, the kingdom has indeed suffered violence and has been hidden in great conflict, as many holy souls who have reached this level of dominion can testify along with me. This serves as a good and hopeful introduction to the ruling Scepter of Love, which will reveal a far more pleasant and joyful kingdom, when all and

<summary>Jane Lead describes her transition from a difficult period of prophetic ministry and spiritual discipline to a new state of "Love Dominion," where Christ rules the inner soul with peace rather than severity.</summary>

Every spirit <note>referring to the various faculties and drives of the human soul</note> is won over and made subject to the Sovereignty of **Love**, arranged in their proper order and place under their superior Governor—namely, the **Heaven-born Spirit**, who represents Christ upon the Throne. Now, when every inner impulse is infused <note>original: "tinged"</note> with the fiery **Balm of Love**, how they will run and fly at the mere look of the Kingly anointed Eye! There, the rays of Love are so intense <note>original: "vehement"</note> that they captivate every thought into obedience to the Lord’s Anointed King—not just in some things, but in everything. This will truly make every saint who has reached this state walk worthily of God, whose very being consists of the essence <note>original: "Essentiality"</note> of Love. Such a person is admitted as God’s companion, living in the central life of a dominion ruled by Love.

From this necessarily follows a holy boldness to enter into a shared inheritance <note>original: "propriety"</note> with Christ before God the Father, as heirs of all kingdoms worth inheriting, whether they are hidden in spiritual mystery or manifested outwardly. The center of **Love** opens through all things and brings in an endless pleasure and joy that no worldly trickery <note>original: "craft"</note> can steal away. Therefore, Love is that most excellent thing which will heal and cure everything within the soul, reconciling all that was in conflict <note>original: "at variance"</note>, whether in relation to God or to our fellow members. 

Therefore, what is more to be desired, or what can be compared to it? I must solemnly declare <note>original: "profess"</note> that I have found such sovereign power and sweetness in the opening of this center of Love that I cannot help but recommend <note>original: "commend"</note> it to others. I earnestly seek to have more fellow-guests <note>original: "fellow-Commoners"</note> with me at God’s table, so that we may sit down together and feed upon the fruits of Love, which are the wine and strength of the Kingdom. 

For in truth, I have come to witness Saint <note>original: "Holy"</note> Paul’s conclusion: that Love is the absolutely perfect thing that removes all imperfection, taking away all sorrow and grief from the mind and heart. For it is a strong and intense heat that consumes fear and anxiety. Oh, how it shelters and secures the soul from all blood-sucking vipers <note>demonic or intrusive negative thoughts</note>, so that they cannot prey upon the soul's inner parts! Because this is a love that cannot conceive <note>original: "imagine into"</note> of any evil thing, no venom of the Serpent can mingle with it. This causes the soul to know that it has returned to its original <note>original: "primary"</note> harmony and **Virgin Simplicity** <note>a state of pure, uncorrupted spiritual focus, often associated in this period with the soul's original state before the Fall</note>.

But now it may be asked: *Do I offer this praise of such high and perfect Love based on revelation, or from—*

...divine and pure sensation? I must acknowledge it comes from both; they meet together as they proceed from one **Love Center**. This was the first mirror <note>original: "glass"</note> in which I saw the lovely <note>original: "aimable"</note> beauty and worthiness of this most excellent thing, along with all the advantages and great privileges <note>original: "prerogatives"</note> that attend it. For it brings nothing less than a kingdom of sweet, peaceful friendship <note>original: "Amity"</note> and love, from which I now receive an unspeakable benefit.

But it may be asked further, *How does this most precious and worthy thing come to be so fundamentally <note>original: "essentially"</note> established in the soul that it rules forever upon the throne, having achieved absolute victory over whatever would stir up trouble and rebellion <note>original: "Sedition"</note>?* In answer to this, I shall share my own experience as one who has obtained grace of this kind. It was conceived through spiritual forms <note>original: "Species," likely referring to the sensible impressions of divine things</note> and revelation, and formed within me as a true, essential life-birth, which I found grew and increased daily in wisdom and strength. 

But then, after this love had grown to such an intensity <note>original: "vehemency"</note> toward my God—as he was known to me in the person of my Lord Christ through frequent visits and very intimate conversation, which I enjoyed daily in a mysterious <note>original: "unknown"</note> and most singular way—it was so pleasing <note>original: "grateful"</note> and sweet that it drew my higher life-spirit <note>original: "superior vital spirit"</note> up after him. Consequently, all other places and company became very tedious and unpleasant to me whenever I was prevented from feeding the flame of my love through the beautiful enjoyment of his presence. 

The truth is, I had absorbed <note>original: "sucked in"</note> such a sweet fragrance <note>original: "Savour"</note> through a most intimate friendliness to which my Lord lowered himself <note>original: "condescended"</note> for the joy and love-delight of my soul, that I could hardly bear to give my time or conversation to anyone in whom I could not see his all-lovely <note>original: "all-aimable"</note> spirituality. I reserved myself for him alone. Indeed, I could have willingly attended to this communion day after day, without any night coming between. 

But my **Bridegroom**, after all this winning of my heart <note>original: "ingratiating of himself"</note>, often tested <note>original: "prove"</note> those two great witnesses within me: **Faith** and **Love**. These grew like twins out of the fresh **Sharon** <note>referring to the biblical Plain of Sharon, symbolizing a fertile and beautiful spiritual state; see Song of Solomon 2:1</note>, which was renewed within, from which they sprang with great strength and loveliness. However, they were forced to endure many hard encounters and violent assaults. First, in this respect: my Lord Jesus, having created his own living imprint <note>original: "Signature"</note> in me through his frequent appearances, and thus establishing himself within as an essential rising [Body] <note>The text continues on the next page with "Body"</note>

...body, which naturally cared only to move and act in the *sphere* of *high* and *heavenly* things, and at no time to be found outside of that circle. To be thwarted and hindered in this was a very great trial for me, as I looked only to God in this matter, in whom my *faith* and *love* were exercised. I sought to have all those external freedoms and advantages that might cherish and nurture this *Lily-Birth* <note>The "Lily-Birth" refers to the emergence of a pure, Christ-like nature within the soul.</note> according to its native kind; for it could not agree to go out into the gross, muddy, and infectious air of this offensive *Kedar* <note>original: "Keder"; a biblical reference to a place of exile or worldly darkness (Psalm 120:5).</note>, or worldly region. 

But it so happened that my Lord allowed it to be this way because of many earthly necessities, which forced me to spend my time on things that should have been entirely employed upon a more worthy and significant purpose than serving the physical needs of the body <note>original: "serve the Table of the outward Man"</note>. I looked upon these needs as very low and mean in comparison to that high-born heir <note>The divine spirit born within.</note> whom I carried as an invisible glory under the veil of a humble physical body <note>original: "contemptible Corporeity"</note>. 

And upon this consideration, God tested his own begotten *love* to see whether it would hold out under all the discouragements, restraints, and suppressions it encountered, both inward and outward. For I believe I had such a strong connection to and hold on the Almighty that it would have been an easy thing to have had all necessary things come to serve the free-born spirit, rather than being engaged in the toil and servant-like worry over the basics <note>original: "Rudiments"</note> of this present fallen and selfish generation. My spirit had long since been made to turn away <note>original: "descent from," likely meaning to dissent or distance from</note> from the customs and manners of this generation in obedience to the *Law of Faith*, which I continue to follow, expecting all blessings to flow from it. 

And though until now the Lord has exceedingly tested me in this, yet now a thousand-fold blessing is beginning to break in, even through that pure and unique *dominion of Love* which I feel opening within me with the highest transforming power <note>original: "leavening Property"</note>. For it reaches to the boundaries of the everlasting mountain of God’s unchanging love, from which it springs. And why should it not multiply and produce as it goes on, continuing to conquer all hostility wherever it meets it? Having overcome hostility within its own nature <note>original: "foolish Essence"</note>, it may do the same in others who groan and struggle for this same love, but are still weak in it and thus unable to wrestle down love's opposite. This is, and will be, the distinctive *motto* by which that perfect thing called *Charity* <note>In this context, "charity" refers to divine, selfless love (Latin: caritas).</note> will be known,

...known—the kind that does not seek its own dignity and peace in heavenly places to reign in love alone, but most passionately seeks it for its fellow members, even when it is rejected and fought against. For this is the new Gospel Law, so strongly emphasized by the Lord and his Apostles in their time, as they knew it was the only means to plant us back into our own pure eternal origin, where nothing but perfect love was known. Oh! This is of such importance and weight that I cannot help but describe, from my own experience of it, the extraordinary sweetness to be found in it when it comes from a conqueror who has been tested by God himself and by our fellow disciples—which is more piercing than any trials from strangers. Yet all this will not extinguish the right spirit, which is infused <note>original: "tinctured"</note> with the divine flame of love. It is so strong that it overcomes all attempts to choke it and all floods that would drown it.

Thus, having given a most true and firsthand <note>original: "experimental"</note> account of how the *Kingdom of Love* came to rule supreme; for every shaking of this pure plant only rooted and fastened it like an invincible rock. This applied both to God—the Essence of Love—and toward all my fellow members, who grow in their various degrees within this Body of Love, fulfilling that Royal Law which is *to love one another from a pure heart*. This is not to be done superficially or falsely, but *with such a love as that with which God our Father has loved us*. He caused his love to enter into our hostility <note>original: "Enmity"</note> and thereby killed it, reconciling a depraved nature that stood out in stubbornness <note>original: "Refractoriness"</note> against his grace and kindness. In this same way, we are to love one another, even when we encounter resistance and contradictions <note>original: "Gain-sayings"</note> that try to kill or oppose this pure spirit. Yet *Charity* <note>In this context, divine love or "Agape."</note>, which is born of God in us, will suffer and endure, rising up as a conqueror through all beatings and attempts to extinguish it. 

And truly, whoever arrives at this state and can stay firmly upon the throne—unceasingly reigning over all such assaults and contradictions within and outside of themselves—may truly conclude that the perfect state has arrived. It is this that will redeem them from all evils, even from the wrathful root of hostility, from which all self-love, envy, arrogance, greed, and bitterness grow. Now *Love's Birth* <note>The spiritual birth of divine love within the soul.</note> rises in its great power in the soul, as that

<page-num>( 90 )</page-num>

...the eternal anointed Christ, who has come to condemn sin in our flesh—indeed, the very root and source of all sin. Through this, we will surely experience the falling away of the *Man of Sin* <note>original: "Man of Sin"; a biblical reference to a figure of ultimate rebellion against God, often associated with the Antichrist</note>; for love is what sinks him beyond recovery into his own bottomless lake, from which he can never rise again.

But one might say, "This is truly a rare and wonderful thing, but is it attainable during this mortal life?" Yes, certainly; otherwise, the Lord Christ would never have urged it or commanded it so frequently. He left it as his final command <note>original: "last Charge"</note> and summed up the entire Law of Perfection as loving God and our fellow members. He continued to preach this same doctrine through his Spirit

<margin>
Colossians 3:12.
The Gospel of John, chapters 16, 17; and 1 John 4:7, 8, 9, 10, 11.
</margin>

in the Apostles after his ascension, as is recorded abundantly by St. John and St. Paul. Read their letters <note>original: "Epistles"</note>, and you will find what a great importance is placed upon this gift above all others.

Now the Holy Spirit introduces *Charity* <note>original: "Charity"; in this 17th-century context, this refers to "Agape" or divine, selfless love rather than simply giving money to the poor</note> as something distinct and more excellent than all other gifts. Divine inspiration revealed that *Charity* is of such high origin and pure descent that nothing defiled can ever enter it. Its substance is an invisible body, as thin as air and as piercing as a flame—an all-moving, active, and vigorous spirit of power and life. It moves in and out like an invisible breath that no mortal eye can see. Its force can only be felt as a *seraphic heat* <note>original: "Seraphick Heat"; referring to the Seraphim, the highest order of angels, whose name means "the burning ones," associated with the fire of divine love</note> that burns like a refiner's fire within our physical bodies <note>original: "Corporality"</note>. It does this so that it may infuse our nature with the qualities of the Holy Spirit. 

This is the true and faithful description <note>original: "delienation"</note> of this *perfect* thing that the Apostle calls *Charity*. Wherever this comes, it makes all imperfect things flee. Yet it strengthens and confirms every plant sown by the Spirit, causing those things that have long lain buried to sprout above the ground. It is the "king-flower" of the entire *paradisiacal spring* that protects everything else, transforming <note>original: "transmuting"</note> everything into one complete body of love. This love extends and expresses itself in all spiritual generosity, according to its high nature and character. It is never closed off or selfish regarding what it possesses and enjoys, for it claims no private ownership <note>original: "Propriety"</note> over its lot, whatever that may be. Instead, everything is shared freely with fellow disciples who share the same spiritual birth. This love cannot withhold anything from those in need, for it does not seek its own benefit. Then it also has a...

<meta>This page continues the author’s discussion of "Charity" (divine love) as a transformative spiritual power. <page-num>( 91 )</page-num>

...effective power <note>original: "virtual Power"; referring to inherent spiritual efficacy or virtue rather than something simulated</note>, as those made to inherit the finest riches and substance of the everlasting Kingdom. They are able to convey and freely distribute from this inexhaustible treasury to impoverished souls and to relieve those who are in great distress. This power can act as a high, effective balm to heal the wounded who suffer under the *scorpion-sting of the serpent* <note>a metaphor for the painful effects of sin or spiritual attack</note>. For this love is a ministering flame, where holy passiveness <note>original: "holy Passiveness"; a state of quiet, receptive waiting and openness to God's will</note> and humility serve as the open door for its entry; there, these virtues cherish and feed this love with whatever its current state requires. 

Charity <note>original: "Charity"; here meaning "Agape" or divine, selfless love</note> is furnished with all kinds of provisions and stores; it is God’s steward on Earth, whom he dares to trust with the care of his household—those who suffer from spiritual hunger and nakedness. It is a most certain truth that God will appoint such holy overseers in his new-born Church. They shall recover the Apostolic spirit and power that has been lost from the Earth, and it shall rise like a brighter morning star and multiply into a generation that is entirely lovely, in which God may see his own reflection. 

To this end, the Kingdom and its dominion have already entered into some people and are prospering toward this expected level. They are now storing up a full reserve for that day when our *Son of Man* will appear again in the heavens to deliver the Kingdom to the Father, so that God—who is love—may be all in all. But in order to accomplish this final, finishing mystery, a *Melchizedek* priesthood <note>original: "Melchizedeck Priesthood"; a spiritual order of priesthood based on the biblical figure Melchizedek, representing an eternal and royal office superior to the old law</note> shall arise. These priests will know the way into the "Holiest of All" <note>the inner sanctum of God's presence</note>. We will speak further on this after this subject is finished, for it grows out of the *root of love* within us. 

This is the clearest and most undeniable witness of truth: when light, revelation, and the life of spiritual enjoyment meet together, then the declaration of truth carries power and authority with it. But to return to our current topic: what more can be said of it, so that, if possible, the reign of love might spread itself like a net to gather souls for the priestly kingdom?

Other questions <note>original: "Motions"</note> may be further urged, such as: 

*“Would you always want to be in a serene, quiet, and peaceful state of mind, so that no changing circumstances, hardships <note>original: "working-Cross"</note>, or turbulence directed at you—whether from non-believers, foreigners, or fellow believers <note>original: "Gentiles, Barbarians or Israelites"</note>—could cause any disturbance?”* 

This state is possible where charity is dominant. And why is this? It is truly marvelous never to be perplexed and never to be put out of harmony!

<meta>This page addresses an objection to the author's previous claims about the peace found in Divine Love: if this love brings peace, why did Christ suffer agony? The author explains that Christ’s suffering was necessary for his human experience and victory, and that human grief is a sign of spiritual immaturity that will eventually be overcome by the "Law of Love."</meta>

Objection. *But how can this agree? For our most perfect Pattern <note>referring to Jesus Christ as the ultimate example for humanity</note> was said to be troubled, and his soul was "sorrowful even unto death"; and it is also said that "afflictions are not joyful for the present"?*

*First*, we will *answer* this main objection regarding Christ our Lord in his agonies. You must understand that all of this occurred before his Resurrection [take note of that], and it was to show that he possessed the felt passions of our humanity. On our behalf, he had to feel a sinless grief and sorrow and be truly touched by these kinds of infirmities <note>original: "Infirmities"; physical or mental weaknesses/frailties</note>; otherwise, how could he have provided for us through the conquest of Love? If he had not experienced temptation and faced all kinds of contempt and slander, he could not have maintained his own peace—which was the fruit of Love—through it all, nor been reconciled to all the difficult circumstances <note>original: "cross Dispensations"; events or hardships that seem to work against one's desires or the natural order</note> that met him in the world. For it was this perfect Love that carried him through to bear and suffer all things. This served as an example and encouragement for us during our own human immaturity <note>original: "humane Minority"; a legal term for being under the age of adulthood, here used to describe the state of being spiritually underdeveloped</note> when we express those felt pains and passions. But he emerged as a victorious champion and tells us that he has overcome; he assures us that in him we shall have a most fixed and firm peace, even though we are tested by tribulation in this world.

So, from this it must be admitted and allowed that during the time of our immaturity—before Love has risen to its perfect level—it cannot be expected to be otherwise: we may be prone to grief, sorrow, agitation, fears, and anxieties. We may find ourselves moving up and down in an uncertain way, tossed back and forth in our minds. All of this happens because Love is still small, weak, and underdeveloped; it has not yet ascended to the throne of dominion so as to govern everything by the *Law of Love*. Yet, such people should not be hopeless or of a doubtful mind, despairing of ever seeing the "Resurrection of Love" or possessing its peaceful protections <note>original: "Immunities"; exemptions or protections from the disturbances and trials of the world</note>.

Know, whoever you are, that if you are made willing to risk the loss of everything to arrive at this "Pearl of Charity" <note>referring to the "Pearl of Great Price" from the Gospel of Matthew, symbolizing the supreme value of the divine</note>, you shall most surely find it; and there will be great rejoicing at the finding of this "lost coin" <note>original: "lost Groat"; a groat was a small English silver coin; the author is referencing the Parable of the Lost Coin in Luke 15</note>. Indeed, the "house" of the soul must first be thoroughly searched, and natural self-love must be swept out. For what is it that we must lose, but only rubbish, filth, and waste <note>original: "Dross"; the impurities that rise to the surface of molten metal during refining</note>? When our hearts are emptied and cleansed of all these, the King of Love may enter in with his full

<summary>The author explains that Christ's human suffering does not contradict the peace of Divine Love, but rather demonstrates his victory over it. He encourages readers that while they currently struggle with spiritual immaturity and emotional turmoil, they can find perfect peace by purging "self-love" from their souls.</summary>

<keywords>Christ's Agony, Resurrection, Law of Love, Pearl of Charity, spiritual immaturity, self-love, purification, peace</keywords>

*But here it may be asked: What is this Love, which is praised so highly above all heavenly and earthly things? Surely there must be some rare, transcending quality in it—one not commonly known—that makes you urge its importance so passionately.*

*Truly, I have good reason; yet it is not I, but Love itself that moves me to reveal its mystery. Fortunately, this mystery has not been understood until now, through the opening of its fountain so that it may flow out abundantly. For sharing the light of this knowledge only prepares the way for the substantial reality of Love itself to rise tangibly in its flaming quality.* Take this further account of it: it is known to be the most holy and pure offspring <note>original: "Generation"</note> of the *immaculate Virgin of God* <note>In mystical theology, the "Virgin of God" or "Divine Sophia" represents the feminine aspect of God's wisdom and purity</note>, for which is prepared

( 94 )

...prepared a Virgin-Womb, so that this Birth might be the chosen and undefiled One of her who brings it forth. It will not be the same now at Christ’s second Birth as it was at his first. At his first birth, his face <note>original: "Visage"</note> was marred more than any man’s; he was a Man of Sorrows and acquainted with grief, and there was no beauty or attractiveness <note>original: "Comliness"</note> in him. This caused him to be disregarded <note>original: "set at naught"</note> and greatly despised, even though he was without sin or deceit. But because he was found to be a sufferer under the weight of sin, he could not be exalted until he had destroyed <note>original: "slain"</note> the enmity and achieved victory. This is how he has his first birth within us, which must come before his second birth. 

But there is a vast difference between Christ’s first and second coming. Many ages have passed in which the saints have only known the *birth of Christ within them* in this weak, suffering, dying, and shameful <note>original: "reproachful"</note> state. The Apostles—upon whom the *Holy Spirit* <note>original: "holy Ghost"</note> was poured out in a most remarkable way, and through whom Christ gave witness of his victory and exaltation by enabling them to work miracles—nevertheless did not progress beyond the *suffering birth of Christ within themselves*. They only had the revelation and foresight of his appearing *in power and great glory*. They passed away from this life and did not see the sign of the Son of Man take up residence in them for a visible reign and kingdom that might redeem them out of all suffering <note>original: "Tribulation"</note>; and so it has continued until this day. 

Yet, there is still no generation found to be as prepared and ready for the *second coming of Christ* as they were in that day. This might make us doubt whether the coming of Christ is truly near, because this *Birth of Love* is so rarely found in anyone—at least, not risen to a position of absolute power <note>original: "Dominion"</note>. Such a birth is a certain introduction to the all-powerful reign of Christ on this visible earth, within the inner qualities of the *flock of Love*. 

But you might say, "*The Apostles, and those who were converted by their ministry, were all living in the dispensation of love and expected the kingdom to be restored to Israel in their own day; what, then, was the reason that Christ’s second birth did not arise in them?*"

To this I answer: It was considered appropriate <note>original: "thought meet"</note> by the Father of Wisdom that everyone of 그 generation should drink from their Lord’s cup of suffering and death, as he often hinted to them while he was personally with them. To these souls, the Lord may say: *“My dove, my fair one, my undefiled, come away; the scarlet dye of love has washed your blackness away, so that now you are completely beautiful in my sight.”* 

This is a prophecy that is now being fulfilled in some people, and it will be fulfilled even more abundantly as this spirit of burning love takes possession of hearts. Then the very face of God will be the covering through which we shall see and know what we were previously ignorant of regarding our own pure birth into love <note>original: "Love’s-Nativity"</note>. <meta>This page transitions from the ecstatic experience of divine love to the formal spiritual structure of the "everlasting Priesthood of Christ," describing it as the highest state of spiritual attainment and a ministry of intercession.</meta>

<page-num>( 99 )</page-num>

->Love is the Lamb's pure Virgin-Bird;<-
->She is all fair and comely in his sight,<-
->So sweet, so mild, and ravishing in his eye,<-
->That in his love-embraces she must lie,<-
->There possessing such pure heavenly <note>original: "Cœlestial"</note> joy,<-
->As none can know, nor any come to annoy.<-
->A bed of rest is found for her here,<-
->That makes Love's trumpet ever sound.<-

Having passed through Love's region and dominion, we have come to set forth (according to the gift still bestowed upon us) the glorious state of the everlasting priesthood of Christ. This is carried out in his most holy temple, body, and heavenly sanctuary, which is built for it by the love-power and wisdom of the Most High. He desires to have a kingdom of priests elected and set apart for this purpose: *to draw near in full assurance of being accepted by him in all their holy offerings, each one from their consecrated tabernacle, to minister to the mighty Jehovah <note>The Hebrew name for God, emphasizing His eternal and self-existent nature</note>.*

For this, a special ordination is required, which no one is capable of receiving except those who are first purified in the burning springs of love. In this way, they may become holy, merciful, and compassionate, interceding for the ignorant and for those who have turned aside from the path <note>original: "tract"</note> of the Just and Perfect One. Now, to be called and anointed by God to this holy role <note>original: "Function"</note> is the greatest spiritual dignity and honor that can be granted to the "love-flock." It excels both the prophetic and kingly dominion. While all of these roles essentially agree with one another, the priestly office outshines them in glory; it is the final and concluding ministry <note>original: "Ministration"</note> which shall reconcile and gather everything into unity with the most holy and ever-blessed Trinity. There, a permanent residence <note>original: "fixation"</note> within the temple-body of the Holy Spirit <note>original: "holy Ghost"</note> shall be enjoyed forever.

Now, in order to reveal and explain this high and unique priesthood, it is appropriate that I should declare the living form <note>original: "figure"</note> in which it appeared to me—or rather, to a part of me which was raised up to be like a clear, burning lamp of love. This part was capable of seeing this great and marvelous vision, which opened from the very essence <note>original: "Beeing"</note> of the Godhead, in whose body of light and glory this

<summary>The author describes the "everlasting Priesthood of Christ" as a supreme spiritual state beyond even prophetic or kingly power, accessible only to those purified by divine love. This priesthood serves as the final stage of reconciliation between the soul and the Trinity.</summary>

Priesthood in the heavens was clearly manifested to my eyesight, which is the foundation I shall build upon, according to the direct pattern <note>original: "express"</note> which the great *Melchizedek* Priest <note>Melchizedek is a biblical figure (Genesis 14, Hebrews 7) who serves as both king and priest; in mystical tradition, he represents the eternal priesthood of Christ.</note> has set open and made understandable here for the benefit and service of that holy and royal tribe that shall be numbered among them. For in the present age, the most precious souls have their call to come out from what is vile, temporary, and soul-cankering <note>original: "Soul-cankering"; something that eats away at or corrupts the soul like a disease.</note>—which works against Love and focuses on earthly things. This call also applies to the more refined religious outward forms (where the viper of sinful hypocrisy has often had a fair covering spread over it) in every changing ministry that focuses only on an external holiness. Until there is a departing from this and an entering into the substance of Love, one can have no part in this unique and royal priesthood. But let us go on to describe it according to the *Mount Zion pattern* which we have seen, and the words that were heard from there.

### *The Vision of the Heavenly Tabernacle.*

I was carried in the Spirit to a place where I saw a light of flaming glory unfolding and opening itself, becoming larger and more visible. In the midst of it was an *abyssal deep* <note>original: "Abyssical Deep"; referring to the unfathomable and infinite depths of the divine essence.</note>, from which there was a working, flowing source in constant motion, flashing up transparent matter. It was so sparklingly glorious in its colors that it seemed as if every kind of precious stone had been mixed into the substance. Then, numerous people appeared, gathered to the glory of this flowing source by the virtue and power that rose out of the deep. This was like the resurrection of pure heavenly bodies—bright and clear—who stood there to receive and drink from this *abyssal deep*. 

Then the "translated spirit" <note>Translated spirit: the author's soul, transported into a higher spiritual state or realm to receive revelation.</note> who saw all this asked, *“What is to be understood by all this?”* And it was answered: *“It is the flowing of the Deity, which is ever generating outward to beget and renew what is like its own being. The sparkling matter that continually flashes out is to consecrate the heavenly order of kingly priests. This will be the very glory of the tabernacle upon them, for this priestly kingdom is to exist under the highest degree of anointing. And whoever can, in a pure and abstract way, come up and lie near the flowing of this abyssal deep, are...”*

In this *vision*, no formal *temple, tabernacle*, or *altar* could be seen. I did not see a *specific Christ* here, except as he was said to be the *Head* and the power that made them shine like so many burning lamps before the throne of the Father's all-piercing eye of glory. There were different ranks among this *holy priestly fellowship*, some in a higher and others in a lower order. But the *testimony of truth, love, and purity* flowed through them all as one entire *Ark of the Heavenly Body* joined together. They sounded forth *new* and wonderful *laud* <note>original: "Laud"; hymns or songs of praise.</note> and *praises*, different from all others who worshiped the *Lord God Almighty* in the heavenly places.

This was a vivid expression and manifestation of the *everlasting* and *unchangeable priesthood* which the *Holy Spirit* will build up and appear within. Therefore, the Spirit has come beforehand to reveal this scene and to provide rules to guide the way to it. This is based on the substantial foundation that opens from the *central eternity*, the source from which all perfect things must spring forth again in this faded and corrupted world <note>original: "Region"</note>. For this purpose, a most *holy* and *royal priesthood* is ordained to be the *salt* of the *earth*, sending out their life-giving seasoning where decay <note>original: "Putrifaction"</note> leading to death has previously reigned. Now we shall proceed and share what has flowed in like a *fountain* sending out its *springs*. It is assured to us by an *irreversible covenant* that a *kingly priesthood* shall stand upon the earth in these latter days. A *choice* and *call* from the *Holy Spirit* is going out to accomplish this. Only the Spirit knows where the true-born *Nazarites* <note>The Nazarites were biblical figures consecrated to God through specific vows of purity (Numbers 6).</note> are, and where those sanctified in the *water* of *life* reside. They will be brought out as *baptized ones* into this *holy priestly order*. Thus, it will be evident to everyone who they are, and all will acknowledge their *priestly royalty*, each according to their various order and degree of power and eminence—

Faith through the operation of God, which opens from the springs of love, is spread abroad by the Holy Spirit and kept as a living coal. This coal can multiply itself into a strong and intense flame of love, which is the nurturing root that makes faith become entirely fruitful—beyond all external sense and sight. It rests and confides in the creating *fiat* <note>original: "Fiat"; Latin for "let it be done," referring to God's creative command in Genesis 1.</note>, which can call into being those things that do not yet exist and make something out of nothing. We may call this the *riches of faith*, which can create an increase even where no visible matter appears. 

This is no ordinary faith; it is found nowhere except where the Holy Spirit has planted himself as a blessing and a growing spiritual profit <note>original: "Revenue"</note>. Whoever receives this gift and tastes its power will soon stop grasping for and loving earthly and perishing things. They will be compelled to offer and consecrate everything possible for the service and use of the holy, royal priesthood of *Melchizedek* <note>original: "Melchizedeck"; a biblical figure from Genesis and Hebrews representing a timeless, kingly priesthood superior to the old law.</note>. 

And although little of this spirit and power appears in the world at this time, know for a fact—by the witness of Jesus—that the long-circling motion of the *holy anointing* (which until now has only scattered sparks of light) is now truly at the dawn of the "Day of Life." What was once only found in sweet movements of love and temporary, passing powers—which even ceased among those who wait at the altar—is changing. 

But we say once more: *Behold, come and see; the burning spiritual <note>original: "ghostly"; in the 17th century, this meant spiritual or related to the soul, not a phantom.</note> body has risen, which will turn all uncertain motion into a fixed act of all-flowing, generative powers.* The Lord has shown me this: *This will first be given to the high priestly order—those who can pass beyond the veil, for whom the "Holy of Holies" stands open.* 

There are different degrees, states, and orders in this final and newly established priesthood, in which the Holy Spirit will be seen as everything. According to the instruction I have received, I will describe this order and how to recognize those called and chosen by God for it. Each person ordained for this will have the "foundation seal" or "priestly mark." This will be a recognizable character that no one can erase or deny. 

Regarding outward gender, there will be no distinction, even though the symbolic <term>Typical Priesthood</term> <note>original: "Typical"; referring to the "types" or foreshadows found in the Old Testament, which the author believes are fulfilled and replaced by spiritual reality.</note> admitted only males in its day. ...both become one, with everything being absorbed into the newness, strength, power, and glory of his own emerging new birth. This is just as it is witnessed in Scripture: *Where there is neither male nor female, but Christ is all, and in all.* <note>Galatians 3:28 / Colossians 3:11</note> He is the life-giving <note>original: "quickning"</note> Spirit and the true evidence that will reveal itself according to each person's level of spiritual maturity and growth. In this state, individual personalities matter little; the purity of wisdom and the power of the Spirit are all that God respects in his priestly offerings. We must understand that these priests belong to three ranks or orders, established and ordained by the great High Priest <note>Jesus Christ</note>. He continues in his mediating kingdom until the entire work is completed for our mutual glorification with him.

The first and lowest rank consists of those who have not yet moved beyond the Son's ministry. These people are still conscious of *sins* and *transgressions* committed under the first covenant <note>the Old Testament Law</note>, following the pattern of *Adam’s transgression* that stems from that corrupt lineage. For them, it is necessary to undergo various vigils, separations, atonements, and sprinklings of blood. This qualifies them to enter the circle of *Mount Zion*, where no sin offering is required. However, to rise to the successive levels of the *Melchizedek Order* <note>a higher, eternal priesthood mentioned in the Book of Hebrews</note>, a total burnt offering is required. The "goat" <note>representing the sinful nature or the sacrificial animal of the Old Testament</note> must die, and the infected, sinful life must expire within the flames of the altar. 

This type of offering must be continuous until the entire body of sin is consumed. As long as any part of that nature remains and operates in its circular motion, new atonements, peace offerings, and blood-sprinklings are required. This continues until the "one offering" itself is sacrificed once and for all, which clears away all sin and creates prepared, suitable vessels. These individuals then reach the first level of purity. With their consciences purged, they can approach God with boldness and full assurance of faith, being cleared of the consciousness of sin through the anointing that is poured out to cleanse from *all sin*. This reconciles them and settles all accounts with God—who is most holy and the justifier of those who share in Christ’s roles as prophet, king, and priest. For wherever the Lord offers himself up through the eternal Spirit, he does so through his own "body" <note>the church or community of believers</note> who are being fully perfected. Just as under the *Levitical ordinances* <note>the religious laws prescribed for the tribe of Levi in the Old Testament</note> prescribed by—

In God’s plan, it was appointed for the priests only to carry the Ark. Under this Covenant-Ministry, none but the spiritually consecrated priests of this order can carry the living witness <note>original: "Testimony"</note> of Christ's risen and spiritual body. This body goes forth with great power wherever it goes, causing rocks to break, islands to flee away, and waters to divide. All of this prepares the way for being brought up to the final step of the all-encompassing priestly throne, to sit down with Christ in heavenly places at the right hand of the Majesty on high <note>Hebrews 1:3</note>. Here, we must consider those of this holy, high, and set-apart order who have themselves become the Tabernacle, Ark, and Altar, and the most inward holy place. In them, Christ the Anointed has so poured himself out that he is known as none other than God the Holy Spirit. He performs and accomplishes the most holy service in the person of the Great High Priest. Having perfected holiness, he appears openly in the most inward, set-apart place where God is manifested. No spirit or soul can ever come to know or meet God except here, just as we hear from the mouth of the Lord himself, who told *Moses* that he would speak <note>original: "commune"</note> with him and appear in his glory in this holy place. 

But alas! Who is able to understand this highly exalted degree of a High Priestly calling at first glance? It is so great and wonderful that it makes the light of all previous ministries seem dark and dim by comparison. It far excels them all, just as the *substance* excels the *shadow*. 

But before we proceed to describe this third and final degree that brings one so close to God, let us look further at the two lower orders belonging to this priesthood. What are their qualifications, and from where were they called? The first rank consists of those mentioned before, who have undergone the purifications of the altar, various washings, sprinklings of blood, and anointing with perfumed oils. Through these, they are made perfect and clean, becoming people who no longer have a conscience burdened by sin. This is because the fountain of the blood of life stands always open—Christ in his bubbling Spirit, through which he maintains a sinless purity. From this group, God chooses and ordains his Tabernacle ministers. They are made fit <note>original: "meet"</note> to perform the holy services: offering up the holy incense and heave-offerings <note>original: "Heave-offerings"; a specific type of Jewish sacrifice where the offering was lifted up to God</note>, keeping all the laws and duties of the Sanctuary, and presenting only what is consecrated to the Lord.

<meta>This page continues the discussion of the three degrees of the spiritual priesthood, moving from the lowest rank of mediators to the second order of consecrated priests who are entirely set apart for divine service.</meta>

Lord, in order to the expelling of every defiled thing. Now, the dignity of even this very lowest degree is great, because those within it are made partakers of Christ. In him, they have become mediators of the New Covenant, in which great strength is found. This is because Christ, in Spirit, has become the surety <term>original: "surety"; a legal guarantor or person who takes responsibility for another's debt</term> of this faultless Testament. This gives them great boldness to plead for the remission of sins on behalf of others who still remain in a sinful, imperfect state. These priests, being ready and free to intercede out of pure, unfeigned love, may eventually rise to the highest level of this divine function.

Now, to mention the service and office of the second order: these are people who do not take this honor upon themselves but are chosen, anointed, and set apart by heavenly ordination. These are those set apart so that God’s sanctuary may be established <note>original: "pitched"</note> in their midst. They are not to depart from this sanctuary because the crown of the anointing oil has been poured out upon them, which is nothing less than *sanctification* in the Holy Ghost. Here, they wait in their perfect, God-like order, unable to look upon any defiled thing; as *David* says, *The Lord has set apart the godly man for himself* <note>Psalm 4:3</note>. 

God claims all who belong to the priestly order for himself; he forbids them from being servants to men. No "divided service" can be argued for here; there is no going back to bury their dead or to be defiled by them <note>A reference to Jesus’s command in Matthew 9:22 to "let the dead bury their own dead," emphasizing total commitment to spiritual life</note>. This was the blessing that *Moses* pronounced upon the tribe of *Levi*: *Let your Urim and your Thummim <term>sacred instruments used by the high priest to determine God's will</term> be with your holy one, who said of his father and mother, "I have not seen them," and did not acknowledge his own brothers or know his own children.* This signifies a "coming out from" and a forgetting of everything related to a mortal birth and all external bonds and obligations. It is like God’s taking up of *Enoch*; a cessation from a worldly way of being immediately follows this transition into the separate order. 

God will allow no one to come near him who looks back to the customs, trade <note>original: "traffick"</note>, and commercial entanglements <note>original: "immercements"</note> of this world. Though such things are lawful for others, they are not for those under this anointing for God. God, who is the highest purity, quickly detects an *Esau*-hunter <note>Esau traded his spiritual birthright for a meal to satisfy his immediate hunger</note> or a worldly *Demas* <note>A companion of the Apostle Paul who eventually abandoned him because he "loved this present world"</note>, with whom the Holy Ghost can have no communion. Nor will God take any to officiate in the holy priestly ministry until they are separated from these coarse and servile things.

<meta>This page addresses the practical concern of how those who leave secular callings to serve as spiritual priests will be provided for, leading into a discussion of a hidden divine treasury and the eventual reign of Melchizedek.</meta>

<page-num>113</page-num>

But now an objection follows: *How will those be provided for who must release themselves from all secular jobs? Since they have no ownership in material wealth <note>original: "Mammon"</note>, stock, or supplies, yet still have physical bodies that need temporal things, what way do you propose for their outward survival?*

Regarding this, we refer you to what the Almighty Provider and Overseer has established as a standing rule throughout the holy priestly generation: that God himself is to be their portion and inheritance. Therefore, they were to have no land inheritance among their brothers; the sacrifices made by fire to the Lord God of *Israel* are given to them as their share. How is that to be understood? It means that those who wait at the altar are to receive from the gifts offered at the altar, which no one else has the right to share. 

There is a deeper meaning in this than we are yet permitted to reveal. We can only give a glimpse in these dark times: there is a hidden vein running underground within the holy place that belongs to the priests' income. The vulture's eye <note>A reference to Job 28:7, describing a path unknown to even the keenest-sighted birds.</note> has not seen it, nor can any human craftsman find this golden mine. The everlasting, precious Rock covers it and will continue to do so until the third rank of this high priestly order is revealed through the conquering and sealing power of the Holy Spirit. Then you will see what power the rod of *Aaron* the High Priest has to rule and pierce this long-hidden vein, from which an inexhaustible treasury will flow. 

At that time, gold and silver will be held in little regard. Those who hoarded <term>original: "Ingrossers"; those who buy up goods to create a monopoly or hoard wealth</term> them will be tormented when they see their currency become obsolete. For the great *Melchizedek* <note>A biblical figure representing a priest-king, often seen as a precursor to Christ.</note> will be the absolute monarch and will stamp a new coin that will be used throughout all the earth. 

But whatever this secret is that we have seen in the *Mirror of Wisdom* <note>original: "Wisdom’s Glass"</note>, it is not yet our time to disclose anything further. Instead, let us proceed to the highest level in this Tabernacle <note>A sacred dwelling place for God’s presence.</note>, to settle within the Holiest of Beings, making use of every present advantage through a pure *Nazarite* dedication <note>A "Nazarite" was someone consecrated to God by a special vow of separation and holiness.</note>. Observe only this: if we feel an impulsive power from the Holy Spirit within ourselves, strongly carrying us toward this, it will be a sign of divine ordination and a call to this purpose. We need no human approval or installation into this holy *Melchizedek*-order, in which Christ is to be known.

This was to carry on their *apostolic administration* <note>original: "Apostolical Dispensation"</note>.

> *For we doubt whether anyone since the days of the Apostles can have such a pure and clear discovery of the Mind of God through vision or revelation.*

She constantly threw out whatever could not withstand the touch of the burning stone <note>A mystical reference to spiritual purification or the "philosopher's stone" in a Christian context, representing the testing power of divine purity</note> which *Wisdom* had placed in the center of my heart. I want them to know how to distinguish the true revelation of the *Spirit of Christ* from those mixed spirits that are prone to inject their own impulses <note>original: "Motions"</note> and so muddy the pure springs of revelation. 

For you must know, it is a more delicate <note>original: "nice"; meaning precise or difficult to discern</note> and difficult matter to recognize the Spirit’s speech and voice in this time since the Lord Christ’s ascension than it was before. In those days, the *Word of the Lord* came expressly to the patriarchs and prophets, particularly by an immediate voice. God was very familiar in His way of speaking, as He was with *Abraham* and *Moses*, and so on through the prophets until *Christ’s* time. *But now, in these last days, He speaks to us through the Spirit of His Son, which is the Spirit of Truth and the Comforter.* That is the Spirit we are to test—and to be tested by—to see whether or not He has entered into us as a Spirit of prophecy and revelation. You shall know this first by the preparation of the inward mind; for the speaking Word or Oracle of God must have a most holy, separate place, distinct and apart from the outward man. An inward temple-body must be prepared; otherwise, there can be no expectation of the holy Ghost,

But it may be further questioned: *How can this temple-body be raised up and kept so spiritual that it remains distinct and separate from the mortal body, with its physical senses and earthly impulses?*

**I Answer:**

Here indeed lies the great *mystery* of all *mysteries*: how to keep the spirit of the outward birth <note>The "outward birth" refers to the physical, carnal nature of a human being</note>, with all its infecting distractions <note>original: "multiplicities"</note>, from rushing into the inward court where the soul and God are to be in private counsel together. For if there is a breaking in of the troubled sea of thoughts, this is what adulterates the pure revealed things and brings a false covering upon them. 

Therefore, I shall provide these following rules, as they were given to me by the holy anointing <note>A reference to the guidance of the Holy Spirit (1 John 2:27)</note>. When I first sought this privilege of divine inspiration, I was counseled, regarding the outward state of things, *to avoid, as much as I could, the choking, entangling cares of this life, and also to sequester myself from all worldly conversation, and give up spirit, soul, and body in pure dedication.* I can say that, in my own measure, I have been obedient to this, and I have found that good things for the spiritual part of me have grown <note>original: "accrued"</note> by it. I shall never have cause to repent of this, no matter what my losses may be regarding my worldly lot. 

Now the next rule, which is more interior and evangelical, concerns the inward and holy place of the mind: *For this reason, take more special care to keep the outward birth at a distance from the superior and inward birth; call in your heavenly guard over it. Enter into no negotiation <note>original: "Parley"</note> with the outward man of reason <note>The "man of reason" represents human logic and intellect, which the author views as inferior to spiritual revelation</note> concerning spiritual matters, for you will find him to be a false prophet, in no way consistent with this ministry the soul is waiting upon; therefore, watch and remain in a defensive posture against all invasions of this kind.* 

Then again, take a further caution concerning a finer, more subtle sort of imagination <note>original: "imaginariness"</note> which comes in the appearance of spirituality. Here lies the greatest danger of mistake, when seemingly good impulses arise and spring up, yet come only from the *astral kingdom* <note>In mystical thought, the "astral kingdom" refers to a realm of psychic influences and stars that can mimic divine revelation but is not the ultimate truth</note>. They are sometimes so divine that you can hardly distinguish them from the true teaching of the holy anointing. There are spirits that will put on such a priestly dress, like those who rose up against *Moses*, the Lord’s infallible prophet, saying, "*Are we not spiritual, and therefore may be admitted into the secret of God with you, and there give our verdict as from the oracle of God?*" Thus we are in danger of being surrounded by spirits so finely clothed that they would be very offended <note>original: "think much"</note> to be refused, but

<meta>This page continues the author's exploration of divine revelation, specifically contrasting the experience of being fully "taken up" into the Spirit versus receiving the Holy Spirit within the soul's natural faculties, while warning of the risks of self-interference.</meta>

<page-num>127</page-num>

It was as much as if he had said that it was neither a human nor a mortal day that rose and surrounded him, but the one everlasting Day, or the Ancient of Days <note>original: "Ancient of Days"—a title for God from the Book of Daniel, symbolizing eternal existence</note>, in whose light he had the glorious vision of heavenly things themselves. Thus, he was "in the Spirit," which served as his defensive power and guard to keep out all other trivial spirits. 

Observe this further: it is a very different thing for the mind and spirit of the soul to be caught and taken up into the Spirit purely and abstractly; for then it is secured from mixtures. However, when the Holy Spirit enters only into the natural faculties <note>original: "Property"—referring to the specific qualities or characteristics of the individual soul</note> of our souls—giving forth lustrous light and revelation and speaking fluently—it is still at risk of being twisted by our own self-promoting nature. This self-nature is ready and eager to intrude upon divine inspiration as soon as it arises from its own deep center. 

I speak this from experience, having traveled through deep and winding paths <note>original: "Meanders"</note> before I came to know the true and certain way of *revelation*. By this, I am now freed from all doubt concerning the *true Oracle speaking within the soul* in my own particular case. I watch over this with care to keep out all fluttering spirits that are born and nourished in the *astral and elementary region* <note>original: "astral and elementary Region"—in the author's cosmology, the physical and planetary realms which can produce deceptive or lower-level spiritual influences</note>. These spirits can come and go to test us until we have moved beyond their *kingdom*. 

Indeed, when the soul's spirit experiences such an uplifting into the Spirit—like the beloved disciple <note>A reference to John the Apostle receiving the vision of Revelation on the Isle of Patmos</note>—it is then beyond all fear. But this is very rare; only high favorites frequently experience such *transportations*. The other experience, however, is more common and frequent among the saints who love and enjoy that spiritual service <note>original: "Ministration"</note>. For their sake, I have been moved to reveal these secret things so that they may put on their impregnable strength of defense. This allows them to preserve this great immunity—the choicest of all God's favors—in its incorrupt state, as if from the pure spring of the Spirit. 

Then you will find every faculty infused <note>original: "tinged"</note> with the *fiery glance* of the *Holy Spirit*. Just as you see a dark and hard piece of *iron* placed in a *fire* is so saturated by it that it becomes one and the same with the *fiery flame*, you must keep and nourish your spirit there like the *salamander* <note>In alchemy and folklore, the salamander was a legendary creature believed to live in and endure fire</note>, which always lives in the *fire*. In this way, the Holy Spirit, with his invisible flaming body, penetrates the soul's spirit until it is surrounded by light. Like the angel described in Revelation 19:17 as *standing in the sun*, the soul appears as the very sun of glory. 

Therefore, it is highly valuable for any saint to receive such a clothing of the Spirit. In this state, nothing of *Adam's* weak or powerless *nakedness* <note>Referring to the fallen state of humanity after the loss of divine light</note> can be seen; instead, *glory, strength, and power* will be your *dwelling place*. Therefore, these are gifts to be highly valued.

<summary>The author explains the difference between total spiritual transportation and the internal influence of the Holy Spirit, warning against the intrusion of "self-promoting" or "astral" spirits. The soul is compared to iron in a fire, becoming one with the divine flame until it is clothed in glory rather than the weakness of fallen humanity.</summary>
