...and desired, rather than slighted or despised. Now that I have given a clear and firsthand <note>original: "experimental"</note> account of this subtle <note>original: "nice"—meaning precise or subtle in this context</note> and difficult point, which I hope has reached the satisfaction of those who are doubtful, I shall now—by virtue of a heavenly commission given to me by the Lord—offer counsel and caution to three groups and levels of people.

The first of these are those who question *whether there is any Spirit of Prophecy or Revelation given since the Apostles died, believing that all such gifts died with them.* This would be a sad and deplorable thing if God, since that age, had cut off the spring of *revelation* from its source. If so, the sheep and lambs of Christ’s flock could no longer expect to be fed from the fresh springing pastures where no one has yet stepped, or to drink from those flowing rivers of life that are renewed daily from the fountain of the Godhead. Let such people remember and consider the many biblical prophecies and promises concerning the continuation of this gift until the very end of time, in both the Old and New Testaments. I shall mention only a few from the latter: *John 14:16–18; 2 Corinthians 4:8; 1 Corinthians 2:10–14; Joel 2:28 <note>original: "John 2. 28"—likely a typo for Joel 2:28, the famous prophecy regarding the pouring out of the Spirit</note>; and Hebrews 8:9–11.* Many more scriptures than these could be called upon to confirm the continued flow of the Spirit throughout all ages. Christ comforts us through his Spirit, saying, *"Behold, I will be with my own to the end of the world."* Now, speaking on behalf of the Lord, I beg you not to eclipse the light of the *Morning Star* <note>original: "Day-star"—referring to 2 Peter 1:19</note> in your own souls, nor quench the Spirit, nor despise prophesying. Then the witness of Jesus may rise within you to confirm this most glorious *ministry* <note>original: "Ministration"</note> as a burning *Lamp of Revelation*.

A word of loving caution is for a second group of people: those who have entered this ministry in its first degree and are under the *Spirit’s* discipline and teaching. The word of counsel from the Father of Spirits is this: they must keep precisely to the rules and laws of the Spirit. For from such people, a more radiant and extraordinary way of life <note>original: "Conversation"—in the 17th century, this meant one's entire conduct or lifestyle</note> will be expected. This is shown through a self-denying life, dying to all those mortal things and sensual pleasures that strangle and choke the fiery breath of the Holy Spirit. These things prevent the Spirit from kindling further and deeper within the soul’s essence, so that there may instead be a growth toward a mighty increase and fruitfulness in the love and unity of the Spirit. We should have all tenderness to support, love, and cherish every "olive plant" <note>likely a reference to fellow believers or new spiritual gifts</note> that springs out of this *Edenic garden*, which is watered by the holy mists and dews of the Spirit.

Now, this word of wisdom and counsel greets the third group, whom we perceive to be of a high and lofty stature, ha—

In hopeful expectation of this, I have been compelled to disclose these great and deep secrets. <meta>This page serves as a concluding postscript and a list of corrections (errata) for the preceding treatise, transitioning from the prophetic tone of the main text to a more humble, instructional address to the reader.</meta>

This postscript, along with the *list of errors*, is addressed
To all who are learned in verse or prose.
These *verses*, *rhymes*, or whatever you wish to call them,
Printed in *italics*—if they flow with any 
smoothness at all, that was never the intent or the goal. It is the meaning
that matters; that is what, if *anything*, holds the preeminence.
Do not look here to find proper poetic rhythm or meter, 
which actual poets focus on, 
and in telling you this, I believe I am being helpful. 
For since the author is within the *Heavenly Cloud* <note>a mystical state of divine communion or spiritual inspiration</note>,
You who have vowed to focus closely on your *studies*
should stand bare-headed and keep your distance, as you are part of the *earthly crowd*.
This is because you do not know the *divine Magia* <term>a term in mystical philosophy referring to the creative, spiritual power of God acting through nature</term>. 
If you did know it—oh, if you only did—then 
you would be better than you are, even if you were already the most pious of men.
For look, through the author, both purity and power 
are expected to descend from above at every hour.
While *visions* are **revealed here, as you can see**,
it is also true that *here the Muses are hidden*. <note>original: "the Muses are conceal'd" — meaning the author is not relying on traditional Greek poetic inspiration</note>
As for the *Graces* <note>referring to both the three Greek goddesses of charm and the general quality of elegant writing</note>, if you are not blind,
you will see you must look *elsewhere* to find them.
For *here* you are commanded to *look inward*: **Please, take this to heart.**

->Your plain, downright, true-hearted friend, to serve you,<-
->**EH. MAMP.**<-

To this end, all* prophecies *that have existed since the foundation of the* world *may have their fulfillment in this last* age. *For this, good* assurance *is given from the fresh springing of the* Word *of* Wisdom, *which may produce a sealed* commission <note>A divinely sanctioned authority or message.</note>, *not only in the written* letter, *but from the sensible, fiery breath of the*

<meta>This page continues Jane Lead’s preface, where she explains the divine origin of her writings. She describes herself as a "subordinate author" who was commanded by a spiritual voice to record her mystical experiences starting in October 1679, despite her initial intention to keep them private.</meta>

*...true **anointing** <note>original: "Unction"; a spiritual blessing or consecration by the Holy Spirit.</note>, which is the living **letter** <note>original: "Epistle"</note>, by which alone I desire to be known to you. From that source, I send a pure greeting of love to those whose ears are opened to hear and receive what the Spirit of Truth has declared and unfolded in this little volume. This book is now presented for public view, contrary to the intent and purpose of its secondary author <note>Lead refers to herself as the "subordinate author," implying that God is the primary author of the text.</note>. While I was waiting in my continued course and order for the heavenly rain <note>original: "showring"</note>, to feed and be refreshed in this all-fruitful and pleasant pasture—being satisfied here to take my time <note>original: "lengths and breadths"</note> in all free conversation with the Lord, the Spirit—I knew him well by his inward, flaming presence <note>original: "Body"</note> which at certain times stirred and rose. Then I could hear and distinctly know the voice of his mouth, which came as a soft, melting breath of fire and said: "Record what has been seen, heard, tasted, and felt since* October 1679, *and do not hesitate to declare it; for it has a specific service to perform on God's behalf, though it is currently hidden from you. Only observe and be watchful, and I will certainly follow close behind this <note>original: "at the Heels"</note>." And thus you may see under what pressure <note>original: "constraint"</note> I am, and by whose commission these deep and mystical revelations are now published. He foresees what effects they will have, and which souls and spirits are to be touched and ignited by the same burning ray, which will surely protect <note>original: "over-shadow"</note> those who believe. And for those who doubt, I have only this caution—and that from the Holy and Just One: that from now on they stop making rash and **unspiritual** <note>original: "uncircumcised"; here meaning judgment made without spiritual insight.</note> judgments, which arise from the human reason and spirit. Above all, they should put on a meek, simple, and childlike spirit, and so stop being harmful to their own souls. For nothing is more damaging to the growing and springing life and light than to give way to **disbelief** <note>original: "Incredulity"</note> and an evil suspicion raised from the dark mists of the senses and worldly wisdom, which can never reach the deep things of the Spirit. Therefore, if there be...*

# To the Living Stones gathered, etc.

These spirits bind and put out the eyes of **Samson the Nazirite** <note>original: "Sampson the Nazerite"; Lead uses Samson as a symbol of the eternal spirit within humanity that is blinded and enslaved by worldly passions.</note>, who represents the eternal spirit found in all the sons of Adam. 

Against this spirit, the great **Apollyon** <note>original: "grand Apollyon"; the "Destroyer" or the devil, mentioned in Revelation 9:11.</note> hatches his treacherous conspiracy. He feels great indignation against us because such love, grace, and goodness have now abounded toward us in Christ Jesus our Lord. Christ has come to offer himself as an effective salvation for everyone; there is not a door he passes by, but at one time or another he knocks at it, seeking entrance to redeem and recover his own eternal spirit from bondage, death, and misery. 

But such are the subtle wiles of that enemy of all mankind that he provides a **Delilah** <note>original: "Dalilah"; referring to the biblical figure who seduced and betrayed Samson, representing worldly temptation.</note> for some, to steal and draw away their minds and to seduce their more noble part toward that which is vile, base, and sensual. Then, he casts out another more subtle bait for those who are disciplined <note>original: "mortified"; those who have suppressed their physical desires or worldly passions.</note> and have escaped those worldly charms. These people have achieved a sober and lawful use of the things of this creation, yet they are still held captive through the many mortal cares, businesses, and concerns for the **outward man** <note>The physical body and its material needs, as opposed to the spiritual "inner man."</note>. Their spirits are so overcharged and weighed down by these things that they cannot rise up to shake off this earthly dust or walk with God in **Enoch’s state** <note>A state of constant spiritual communion with God, referring to the biblical figure Enoch who "walked with God" (Genesis 5:24).</note>. They fail to observe the counsel of the Lord Christ: first to seek the Kingdom of Heaven, and with all your getting, to get wisdom. Most people practice the exact opposite: they make sure of the Beast’s kingdom first, and the perishing **Mammon** <note>original: "Mamon"; a term for wealth or material riches, often personified as a false god.</note> of unrighteousness,

--- Page 70 ---
<meta>This page continues Jane Lead’s preface to her spiritual community, emphasizing that her writings are not products of human intellect but are divinely revealed to guide the soul toward a mystical resurrection and liberation from sin.</meta>

# To the Living Stones gathered, etc. <note>original: "gathered, &c."; "Living Stones" is a biblical reference (1 Peter 2:5) to believers who form the spiritual temple of God.</note>

...naked simplicity, as they were every day revealed and given forth, not in the dress of **human wisdom**. No, they are of another descent, and the spiritual ones in God will feel, know, and taste from which **treasure-house** they proceed. As for others, we do not heed their judgment, but we shall be found in hearty prayer for their true enlightenment, so that all may come to know what the **Trumpet of Love** <note>original: "Love-Trumpet"</note> is now sounding for. Is it not so that both earth and sea may give up their long-buried dead, and that this cursed ground may no longer shut up those who are **dead in Christ**? 

Are these not tidings brought from the **bright East**? <note>In mystical traditions, the East symbolizes the source of divine light and the dawning of Christ’s presence in the soul.</note> They announce that the Lord is coming to bring himself forth in a new and marvelous **resurrection**, such as has never been yet. Therefore, he has beforehand sent his holy **Spirit of Prophecy** to proclaim what he intends to fulfill, according to his ancient purpose and decree. For this end, he has shown what gates are to open to give entrance into this all-fruitful **Sharon**, <note>original: "Sharon"; a fertile plain in Israel, used here as a metaphor for a place of spiritual abundance and peace.</note> where the scattered flock of love has been hunted and chased like panting **deer** <note>original: "Harts"</note>, shifting their pastures and yet not finding where to lie down and be at rest. 

Indeed, this rest can never be expected until you come through the **Seven Seals** <note>A reference to the Book of Revelation; the breaking of these seals represents stages of spiritual unfolding or the removal of internal barriers to God.</note>. There, you shall witness the full, glorious day of resurrection, through all things becoming **new**. In that state, the Holy Spirit—your Overseer and your very sense of life—shall always be known as your permanent foundation in the state of resurrection. 

And though the **Sadducees** <note>original: "Saduces"; Lead refers to those who, like the biblical sect, deny the resurrection—specifically, those who lack faith that the soul can be transformed and perfected in this life.</note> are numerous, those who have no faith that they will ever see this first resurrection, it is a resurrection that makes one free from the **body of sin**. One must first bear witness to this by being born alive from the dead, according to the Scriptures: *You has he **made alive** <note>original: "quickned"</note>, who were dead in trespasses and sins.* 

Therefore, it is not incredible to us that God should raise the dead (see Romans, chapters 4, 5, and 7), but each one in Christ’s order. For allowance must be given for degrees; for just as death occurs gradually, so also is the **resur-** <note>The text breaks mid-word "resurrection," which continues on the following page.</note>

<summary>Lead describes her writings as divine revelations intended to lead the "spiritual ones" toward a mystical resurrection. She argues that this transformation is a gradual process of being made free from sin and entering a state of spiritual rest symbolized by the "Seven Seals" and "Sharon."</summary>

## *To the Living Stones Gathered, etc.* <note>original: "To the Living Stones gathered, &c."; "Living Stones" is a biblical reference (1 Peter 2:5) to believers who form the spiritual temple of God.</note>

...resurrection <note>original: "rection"</note> perfected through received **Acts of Power**. We may see a likeness of this in the **visible sun**, which does not reach its **noonday height** <note>original: "Meridian height"</note> at its first rising. In the same way, the **Body of the Resurrection** does not advance to its full height of **luster and glory** all at once, but by degrees. Therefore, we must allow for **measures and levels** in all things, whether in **dying or rising**. All of these reveal one **eternal mystery of glory** through the **powers of our Lord Jesus**, who will bring everyone, one after another, up to **glory**. 

Now, let each person prove their own work done in God, whether it be in **Death, Resurrection, Ascension, or Glorification**. For these stages are all linked together, though they are not achieved or reached except in their own **order**, moving steadily from the **first step to the last**. <margin>As you may see in the book titled *The Heavenly Cloud*, by the same author.</margin> You will find the way to attain this described according to the measure of the **Spirit**, which was given to **reveal these long-hidden truths** that have lay under the same **seals**. There you will find the **path that leads into death**, and the **release from it** through the **seventh seal**, which opens for the **resurrection**. The **seven thunders** <note>A reference to Revelation 10:3–4, often interpreted in mystical traditions as divine voices or stages of revelation.</note> clearly express this, and so the process continues to the **ascending reign in Christ’s kingdom**. This kingdom is great and magnificent, providing its own **clear proof** <note>original: "signal Demonstration"</note> by opening the **divine magic** <note>original: "divine Magia"; in the context of 17th-century mysticism, "Magia" refers to the spiritual, creative power of God acting through the soul.</note>. 

By this, the **Holy Spirit** comes to act in his own place—**namely** <note>original: "viz."</note>, in the **ascended body of the resurrection**. From there, he goes forth to work his **wonders**, such as have never yet been performed. This will be the **introduction into permanent glorification**, being **centered together in the ascended, triumphant Spirit**. The **glory** of this is attributed to the **Lamb of God**, of whom it is said, *That he is worthy to receive blessing, wisdom, power, strength and glory* <note>A quotation of Revelation 5:12.</note>, for accomplishing such a full and perfect **redemption**. All of this shall be assigned to those **glorified in Christ**, who, in the **royal**, unchangeable **priesthood**...

<summary>This page explains that spiritual resurrection and glorification occur in gradual stages, much like the sun rising to its peak. Lead references her other works and the biblical "seals" and "thunders" to describe the soul's journey into Christ's kingdom and the divine priesthood.</summary>

# An Epistle

...shall be confirmed, as going up from **glory** to **glory**, until they are **swallowed up in glory, power, might, and excellence**. The manner of this is defined as the **conclusion of everything that has been discussed in this book**, serving as the **final testimony** that has been given to me at this time from the **Lord of Glory**. Therefore, **receive these truths in faith and love**, for **this is the day of God’s revelation**. He sends this book forth as a preceding **messenger** <note>original: "Harbinger"</note> who, according to his **promise**, will soon follow to fulfill all the rich and precious **things** prophesied here. Therefore, he calls upon his scattered **stones** <note>original: "Stones"; a reference to the "Living Stones" mentioned on the previous page and in 1 Peter 2:5, representing individual believers who make up the spiritual temple of God.</note> to come together, to **harmonize** in the **eternal light, love, and purity**. He encourages <note>original: "provoking"; in 17th-century English, this meant to incite or stir someone up to action, usually in a positive sense as in Hebrews 10:24.</note> each other to move forward in this fourfold **process** of our **Lord Jesus**, arising out of this long **night of slumber**; for the day has broken, and the **morning star** may be seen. 

Consider what a life of **glory and immortality** you are called to; it is great and wonderful indeed. Yet know that the **east gate** will now open to those who earnestly knock at it, so that it is truly possible to reach all these **degrees of glory**. **Therefore**, let no one doubt, but be girded with the **omnipotence** of **faith**, which will clear the way quickly, leaving all unbelieving **loiterers** behind. 

We have a **race** to run. Some may not have set out yet, others have newly started, and others are a good way into the **race**; but blessed are those who reach the **prize** <note>original: "Price"; meaning the reward or "prize" of the high calling.</note> first. They do so so that they may return to strengthen and encourage their **companions** in the **race**, who may see the **rosy garland of victory** in their hands and the **diadem** <note>A crown or jeweled headband worn as a symbol of sovereignty.</note> **of precious stones** as the **prize** they have won. This is a prize that well deserves to be run hard for—not wasting time by looking back once we have started, but running in such a way that we may obtain it. 

But it may be asked: who and where are those who are so resolute and strong in **spirit** that they will hold out to the end of the **race**? Truly, none can be found to do this except those who have risen with **Christ** from the dead; he is, and must become, the **racer** within us.

<summary>The author encourages believers to pursue spiritual perfection like a race, promising that the "East Gate" of divine glory is now open to those who have faith and follow Christ's internal lead.</summary>
<keywords>spiritual race, glory, immortality, East Gate, faith, Living Stones, resurrection, Christ within</keywords>

*In us*; for all other *legs* we *run* with will soon *tire* and *be weary*; therefore, do *not set out* until *Christ*—your *life* and *strength*—is *risen* like a *giant refreshed* to *run out* this *conquering race*. There you may come to lie down under the *encompassing glory*, as if in an *everlasting pleasant shade*, possessing all those *high* and *wonderful immunities* <note>original: "Immunities"; here meaning spiritual privileges or exemptions from the burdens of the world.</note>, *glories, crowns, joys, gifts,* and *powers* in the *Holy Spirit*. He will *magnify* his *office* <note>original: "Office"; his divinely appointed role or function.</note> by *acting* and *working* entirely within the *body* of the *resurrection*. Concerning this, I shall not expand any further, but *refer* you to what is discussed in the *former revelations and these that follow*.

I shall now *cease* and *conclude* everything in that *springing fountain* from which this bundle of *revelations* did *proceed*. I have a *good hope and certain persuasion* that the *Lord* will accompany his *own testimony* in a *ray of light*, spreading over all the *darkness* of those *minds* that shall *attend to* and *receive* what is written without *prejudice*. Though to *some* it may appear *abstruse* <note>original: "Obtruse"; difficult to understand or obscure.</note>, *deep, and mystical*, and *hard to be understood*, yet to *others* who have come under the *teaching* of the *same anointing* <note>original: "Unction"; a spiritual influence or "anointing" that grants insight.</note>, it will appear *plain and easily grasped*. The *Spirit* will *testify* <note>original: "bear record"</note> with us of what *was, and is, and is yet to come*. 

Now, the *things* yet *to come* are that *powerful and princely reign* leading to *glorification*, which has long been *prophesied of*. It is *now affirmed again* that the *Lord* will *suddenly be upon us* to take unto himself his *kingdom*, in which *nothing* of the *Beast's reign* <note>A reference to the worldly or corrupt powers described in the Book of Revelation.</note> shall *mingle*. Oh, *holy, precious, and elect*, let us not *sleep*, but *set* our *morning-watch*, for *news* <note>original: "Tydings"</note> has come to make all things ready for him. Let it appear who the *wise virgins* <note>A reference to the Parable of the Ten Virgins in Matthew 25, who kept their lamps ready for the bridegroom.</note> are by their making themselves ready to meet the *Lord*—not only for a *visit* or *salvation*, but for an *eternal marriage* and an *uninterrupted nuptial enjoyment* <note>original: "Fruition"</note>. Thus we shall *be ever with the Lord*—or rather, the *Lord* shall be ever with *us* as his dear *bride*, perfectly redeemed from the *earth*, as it is fitting for one so *holy*.

# *The Introduction.*

It was my intention to have suspended, for now, any further sharing of the revelation that continued to follow me. I would have hidden that golden talent <note>original: "Talent"; a reference to the Parable of the Talents in Matthew 25, representing spiritual gifts or resources entrusted by God to be used rather than buried.</note> solely to let it grow in its own invisible foundation, from where it first began, until such a time as it might emerge through the increasing power of the Holy Spirit to confirm the glory of everything previously shown in visions and prophecies. But *Christ*, the shining banner of glory, stood before me and said, "*Keep a record in the journal of this newly-raised life, according to the spiritual path you are following. Know that what has been revealed, and what is yet to come, shall be confirmed by a powerful witness that will overcome all opposition wherever it arises. Therefore, continue and do not stop writing; for it shall be like the clearing of the current dark day, and many shall be drawn to the brightness of its rising, even though it now seems to be obscured as if by an eclipse.*"

In this way, I was ignited anew by the all-piercing Word of my Lord, who knows the purpose for which he moves me to do this. I still argued against it, because I foresaw no way for such high mysteries to be shared, nor any likelihood that they would be received with that love which does not judge; therefore, I would have preferred to hide these heavenly things within their own source. But seeing as it has pleased the Lord to overrule my decision, I shall be obedient to his Word, believing that it may benefit some who are known to him, who will not disregard the preciousness of the Spirit simply because of the humble vessel <note>The "vessel" refers to the author herself, implying that the divine message is more important than the person delivering it.</note> through which it comes. Therefore, I will no longer hesitate to reveal what is of great weight and importance, being fully assured that the Lord *Jesus* will accompany me until I have completed the declaration of this entire mystery.

I feel the divine agent <note>original: "Agent"; likely referring to the Holy Spirit or a divine internal force acting upon the soul.</note> that works powerfully in holy, set-apart souls and minds, pushing forward to prepare the way for what remains to be told regarding the manifestation of that kingdom which the Lord is now about to restore in his saints. Therefore, he provides fresh and vivid ideas of it, so that pure minds may be encouraged to

## The Introduction.

It was my intention to have delayed, for now, any further sharing of the Revelation that continued to follow me. I would have hidden the golden Talent <note>A reference to the "Parable of the Talents" (Matthew 25:14–30), where a servant is expected to invest and increase what is given to them by God.</note> only for it to grow within its own invisible source—where it first began—until the time came for it to emerge through the increasing power of the Holy Spirit, to confirm the glory of all that had gone before in *vision* and *prophecy*. 

But *Christ*, the shining banner of glory, stood before me and said, "*Keep a record in the journal of this newly awakened life, in accordance with the path you are pursuing. Know that what has been and what is yet to be revealed shall be confirmed by a powerful witness, overcoming all opposition wherever it may arise. Therefore, go on and do not stop writing; for it shall be like the clearing of the current dark day, and many shall be drawn to the brilliance of its emergence, even though it now seems to be eclipsed.*"

In this way, I was freshly inspired by the *all-piercing word* of my Lord, who knows the purpose for which He moves me to do this. I still argued against it, however, because I saw no way for such high mysteries to be shared, nor any likelihood of them being received with a *love* that is free from judgment. Therefore, I would have preferred to keep these heavenly things hidden within their own source. 

But seeing that it has pleased the Lord to overrule my decision, I shall be obedient to His *word*. I believe it may benefit some who are known to Him, who will not look down on the preciousness of the Spirit just because of the lowly vessel <note>original: "Vessels sake"; a reference to 2 Corinthians 4:7, "But we have this treasure in earthen vessels," meaning human fallibility containing divine truth.</note> that carries it. Therefore, I shall no longer hesitate to reveal what is of great weight and importance, being well assured that the Lord *Jesus* will accompany me until I have completed the declaration of this entire *mystery*.

I feel the *divine agent* <note>original: "divine Agent"; likely referring to the Holy Spirit or the inner Christ working within the soul.</note> that works powerfully in holy, set-apart souls and minds. It drives me forward to make way for what remains to be told regarding the manifestation of that *Kingdom* which the Lord is now about to restore in His saints. For this reason, He provides fresh and vivid *ideas* <note>original: "Ideas"; in this 17th-century context, these are divine mental images or spiritual concepts rather than just casual thoughts.</note> of it, so that pure minds may be inspired to

Look for its arrival in their **New Earth** and **Heavens**, which are to be adorned with purity as well as with dominion and power—even the rising Body of the Holy Spirit, which must restore all things that have been in confusion. Now, what precedes this preparation has already been recorded by the **Golden Pen**,
<margin>Meaning the book *The Heavenly Cloud Now Breaking*</margin>
which is dipped in the flowing **Unction** <note>original: "Unction"; a divine anointing or the influence of the Holy Spirit.</note>, to which more significant revelations are added by Him who says, *He has come to make all things New, being ready to provide the Father’s promise in the ministry of the everlasting Gospel.* I say, the faithful Witness and Foundation of all mysteries reminded me of His command to His disciples when He was about to disappear from the outward world <note>original: "disappear to the outward"; referring to Christ’s Ascension and the end of His physical presence on Earth.</note>:
<margin>Acts 1:4</margin>
*That they should wait at Jerusalem, the holy city, in harmony, concord, and oneness of mind, with great confidence and love for each other.* The Holy One performs a deeper search in those who, in this last age, are waiting to be endowed with the full, active **Body** of the **Deity**. This will be manifested in a different way than in the days of the Apostles; then, it was given as a gift, coming down like a **shower**, but now, those who shall be endowed with this remarkable blessing must receive it as a birth within them.

The Apostles were to wait at *Jerusalem*, which was a symbol <note>original: "figure"</note> of the **Principle of Light**. In this state, all aspects of nature and personality <note>original: "Natures and Properties"</note> are harmonized to a balanced state <note>original: "equal Temperature"</note>, all faculties are spiritualized and purged of their impurities <note>original: "Drossiness"</note>, and reconciled to each other in a sweet accord of love. It is plainly shown to me that the Holy Spirit will be born in a peaceful dwelling place, where the conquering life of *Jesus*—through His death and resurrection—has first paved the way. He does this by reconciling the conflicting elements of our nature and bringing them into a divine harmony, renewing our nature into a soft and sweet mildness. This causes all spiritual conflict <note>original: "Contrarieties of Spirit"</note> to cease by subduing it to the Light-Principle.

Therefore, it is useless to expect the establishment of that Kingdom which the birth of the Holy Spirit brings to us until this thorough <note>original: "thorow"</note> work is finished, so that we may achieve unity within ourselves and with one another, which is a very high state of perfection. And truly, from this I have been informed why the birth of power has been delayed <note>original: "stuck"</note>, and why so many attempts—fruitless births—have been brought forth. Therefore, do not let our corrupted <note>original: "bribed"</note> senses or the spirit of unbelief delude us into believing that the Holy Spirit will begin its birth in any way other than what has been revealed by the Lord Himself.

<meta>This page continues the description of the spiritual transformation required for the soul to receive the Holy Spirit, focusing on the soul's liberation from the "lapsed state" of sin and its movement into a crystalline realm of light.</meta>

A person who partakes in this gift of God has put on royal liberty and, being clothed with Christ’s essential body, finds themselves unburdened of all sinful weights. The soul moves swiftly within the Circle of Light and runs its races through the unknown spaces and crystalline spheres, where other worlds appear with all their glorious objects, ranks, and orders. These sights necessarily make this world look base and vile to a soul that has emerged naked and free from the original womb—begotten again from the dead and rising in a perfect, clarified body of light—having fully recovered from that desperate, fallen state that would have sunk the soul into everlasting death. 

Thus, you see the means by which the conflicting properties of nature may be harmonized as they were in the beginning: God must become man, and through his consuming, fiery nature <note>original: "Property"; in the mystical tradition of Jacob Boehme, this refers to the fundamental qualities or "desires" that make up spiritual and physical reality.</note> destroy original sin. Sin is the primary engine that moves the "Wheel of Strife" and division within the soul. This fire is God’s chosen instrument when it is set and tuned by his hand. He gave it a being out of Eternal Nature, into which it must eventually be dissolved again, so that it may be a suitable <note>original: "meet"</note> instrument for the Holy Spirit to play triumphant sounds and teach new lessons that have not been understood until now. 

All of this will be fulfilled when the soul is brought forth as a well-tuned instrument—newly strung and prepared—within the deified man <note>original: "deified man"; a person who has been transformed by divine grace to reflect the nature of God, becoming a vessel for the Divine.</note>. In this state, the Holy Spirit moves every part of our nature according to the central wheel in its newly begotten form. This form is set free to run its races in the sphere of light, where weak mortality and its sinful essences are outrun and left behind in the Fall. Do not let this seem strange or impossible; for in the "Deep" <note>original: "the Deep"; likely referring to the profound inner spiritual realm or the abyss of God's nature.</note> a way <note>original: "Mean"</note> is found to bring forth this long-buried seed, which has long waited to arise in its original body of essential perfection and harmonized powers. This is so it may be shown that God has come to move and reveal himself in the covenant of his love, according to the plan pre-ordained in the heart of Christ. Through him, we obtain this resurrection, which leads to all the following glories that the Holy Spirit shall bring. Even so, *Amen*.

This instruction, being so directly from the Lord, carried great weight. It made me resolve to wait upon the working power in my "inward deep" to bring every divided part of my nature into unity. For I was fully convinced that this uniformity must be the temple in which the Holy Spirit will appear for

But it may be objected: *If the conditions are so difficult, it looks like an impossibility to obtain it. This is because nothing of the evil qualities and conflicting properties must remain—yet these are never so completely calmed in anyone that they might not be stirred up into motion again. Please, therefore, let us know what provision has been revealed against this, so that it may create a hope and belief in those who are still in a state of division and strife—the good warring against the evil, and the evil against the good—being divided between the night and the day, just as even the greatest saints are.*

In answer to this objection: the Spirit of the Lord has searched and found a cure. This remedy will bring the broken and divided state of the first human birth <note>original: "Adamical Birth"; referring to the fallen nature inherited from Adam</note> into unity, harmony, and pure liberty. I shall, as well as I am able, present it in the same model it was given to me, as a true looking glass <note>original: "seeing Glass"; a mirror used here as a metaphor for spiritual revelation</note>. By this, I was directed to fathom the deepest foundation of my eternal soul and to find its true, essential properties, as they were derived from the unmixed purity of the one pure element. This pure, eternal, living soul was thus breathed by God into an angelic image and formed into a physical <note>original: "Organical"</note> body from the one pure element—all of which was to be seen in the first man in Paradise. 

But when *Lucifer* triumphed by darting in his hellish fire, it left such a sting that it poisoned all the inward essences of the very immortal part of us. Therefore, the wound is found to be so deep that no medicine can be found to reach it, except for *Christ*, the in-breathed Word. He alone can reach the soul’s original <margin>*Ephesians 1:10*</margin> ground; and being the *Creative Command* <note>original: "Creating Fiat"; from the Latin "fiat," meaning "let it be done," referring to God's power to create by word alone</note>, he alone can make all things new again, according to the highest likeness of the manifest Godhead. 

And this comes first to be known in a fiery *Ray of Love-Light* that discovers where the root of sin lies. So, when the spirit of the soul comes—by the Word of Life—to understand its own origin and by what means it has been corrupted and captivated, it is made completely willing and eager to join with that *Christ* who rises from the *Center-Light* in its own soul. He rises to redeem and reconcile all to himself that was alienated from him in the birth of strife. Thus, *Christ* in us is our *Peace* indeed, who has made of both parts—and of all things—one *New Creation* <note>original: "New Creature"</note>. He has abolished the *Law of Enmity* <note>The spiritual "law" or state of being at odds with God</note>, blotting and rooting out sin by the same degrees as his own righteous nature enters in, which restores all peaceable *Harmony* <note>original: "Concord"</note>. <meta>This page continues the description of the soul’s transformation through the birth of the Holy Spirit. It describes the soul's liberation from sin and its movement into a higher spiritual state, transitioning from a state of inner conflict to a "well-tuned instrument" for God's presence.</meta>

The person who shares in <note>original: "Partaker of"</note> this gift of God has put on **royal liberty**; being clothed with **Christ’s essential body**, the soul finds itself unloaded of all sinful weights. It moves swiftly in the **circle of light** and runs its races through the unknown spaces and **crystalline spheres** <note>In traditional cosmology, these were the transparent layers of the heavens; here used to describe a vast spiritual realm.</note>, where other worlds appear with all their glorious objects, ranks, and orders. These sights must surely make this world look base and vile to a soul that has come naked and free out of the original womb—as if begotten again from the dead, rising in a perfect, clarified **body of light**, and being quite recovered from that desperate, fallen state that would have sunk the soul into everlasting death.

Thus you see by what means the disagreeing properties of nature <note>original: "Properties of Nature"; refers to the various spiritual forces or qualities that make up the soul’s character, which are often in conflict before spiritual transformation.</note> may come to be harmonized as they were in the beginning: **God must become man**, and by His consuming, fiery nature, destroy **original sin**. This sin is the primary engine that moves the **wheel of strife and division** in the soul. This fire is God’s chosen instrument when it is set and tuned by His hand, He who gave it a being out of **eternal nature**, into which it must be dissolved again. This is so that it may be a suitable instrument for the **Holy Spirit** to play such tunes and triumphant sounds, and to teach such new lessons as have not been understood until now. 

All of this shall be fulfilled when the soul is brought forth as a well-tuned instrument—newly strung and qualified—in the **deified man** <note>One who has been transformed by union with God.</note>. In this state, the Holy Spirit moves every property according to the central wheel in its newly-begotten form, which is set free to run its races in the **sphere of light**. There, weak mortality and its sinful essences are outrun and left behind in the **Fall**. Let this not seem strange or impossible; for in the depths of God, a means is found for the bringing forth of this long-buried seed, which has long waited to arise in its original body of essential perfection and harmonized powers. This happens so it may appear that God has come to move and show Himself in the **covenant of His love**, according to the preordination in the heart of Christ. For through Him, we obtain this **resurrection** in preparation for all the following glories which the Holy Spirit shall bring with Him; even so, *Amen.*

This instruction, being so directly from the Lord, carried great weight with it. It made me resolve to wait upon the working power in my **inward deep** <note>The innermost part of the soul or spirit.</note> for the reducing of every divided property to **unity**. For I was fully convinced that this **uniformity** <note>original: "Uniformity"; here meaning an internal state of harmony and oneness among the soul's conflicting parts.</note> must be the temple in which the Holy Spirit will appear for...

...the bringing of all working powers to a heavenly harmony <note>original: "Confort"; meaning concord or agreement</note> and agreement: I focused on this day after day. These storms introduced various temptations—suggesting that such a thing as this could not hold or last, but would suffer its own overturnings and changes. The argument was that nothing less than God can always remain in a balanced state <term>original: "equal Temperature"; a steady spiritual equilibrium or temperament</term>, and that this state was the final result of the Holy Spirit's arrival, rather than a preparation for it. Thus, I was puzzled and tossed back and forth in my mind, as if I were assigned a task that could never be accomplished or carried through to the desired end. 

But the Lord’s word was mighty and powerful, pressing me forward toward this sweet and well-ordered composure of mind—to remain in a balanced state despite everything that might come to discourage me, whether from visible things or from the dark powers <note>original: "dark Principality"; referring to the spiritual hierarchy of evil</note>, which are much more harmful. This is because they, as spirits, can shoot in their arrows before we are aware of them. So, this has been my vigilant practice for some days: to resist whatever would violate that which should remain unshaken in concord and peace.

And therefore, let no one claim a necessity of continuing in that which has brought in confusion and discord, seeing that a redeeming Spirit has come down to renew and gather the lost back into the Divine Being from which they first proceeded—as pure as God is pure. ### The Vision of the New-Jerusalem.

<margin>Revelation 21:10</margin>
The Lord took me away from my mortal self and showed me the location <note>original: "Scituation"</note> of this great glory, and said, *“Behold and see the pattern of the heavenly houses and mansions which make up the New Jerusalem, where the glory of the one and only God fills everything with the essential light and splendor of his divine goodness.”* I shall, as well as I am able, describe what I saw in the heavens of this *Mother City* (for so it was named to me), who is to bring forth her first and free-born children to replenish the new earth and heavens. 

It appeared as a city founded upon one square stone, whose dimensions were very large. It was all paved with bright, flaming colors, appearing like many various sparkling stones inlaid on the great foundation stone, which gave off a luster as if many bright suns were there. Toward the edge <note>original: "Verge"</note> or outside of this square stone, many seraphic <note>angelic beings of the highest order</note> bodies were seen, entwined arm in arm, and several rows of various spiritual forms of different ranks and stature. These rows appeared like the stories of a building, which were carried higher and higher in a square shape until it was incredibly <note>original: "wonderful"</note> high. And over the heads of the most inward glorified bodies, a covering was spread by one who was more translucent <note>original: "Diaphanous"</note> and of greater splendor than all the rest. The height of his person was a head taller than all the others; he called to me out of this most glorious building, telling me to pay close attention to all I had seen.

But here an *objection* may arise, questioning this revealed grace and proclaimed liberty to the "children of the captivity" who are now called to work upon this heavenly building. The objection is this: *That the time is not yet, and that the last seal is not so near breaking open; and who is it that can give a sign as a testimony that this decree has already gone forth?*

To all this, the Spirit of Truth gives this answer (for he best knows what he has worked toward this end in the central depths of some chosen vessels <note>original: "Elect Vessels"</note>, who are not to make any outcry until the mystery is fully finished in them). Know that the time is at hand; therefore, let no one look far away or run out from themselves and neglect their own vintage at home. By this, you shall receive power to ascend through fire and air and continue that most desirable freedom of communion with the *Holy Trinity*. This is so that those who consider no place their rest or home besides me may have passage. Therefore, pursue and watch to drink in this pure Spirit of Burning, which will clear and remove from the way whatever has kept you from ascending into the *Holy City*."

While these words were spoken within me from the *Anointing* <note>The "Anointing" refers to the internal guidance or presence of the Holy Spirit (referencing 1 John 2:27).</note>, I felt a tangible <note>original: "sensible"</note> rising and spreading over my whole heart, head, and body, as if everything were covered with a cloud of sun-heat, giving out light. By this light, I could see what was being done inwardly as well as feel it. Then it was further spoken within me that I should take notice that this was also the bright Garment of the Sun. Such is the nature of this secret flaming matter that it feeds, strengthens, and clarifies a naked spirit all at once. Then I was inquisitive to know its substance or basis <note>original: "Ground"</note>, but the reply was: "*Do not meddle with that; only receive it passively and cooperate with it when it arises. Then walk with and draw in the nourishing fire and air, and when it rests in its own place, rest with it. Be assured it will not leave you until it has centered you in the Deity.*"

This light, flaming cloud encompassing me gave this further revelation to show what the Holy One is about to do in order to lay the foundation of this strong city, in which the mighty God will pitch his tabernacle and settle his rest forever. Under that seal, the most precious things—even that fine, transparent substance—lie hidden, to be revealed at the right time to beautify this divine building.

The objection is this: *The time has not yet come; the last seal is not close to being broken; and who can provide a sign as testimony that this decree has already been issued?*

To all this, the Spirit of Truth gives an answer—he who best knows what he has accomplished for this purpose in the deepest centers of certain chosen individuals <note>original: "Elect Vessels"</note>, who are not to speak out until the mystery is fully completed within them. Know that the time is at hand. Therefore, let no one look far away or search outside of themselves, neglecting their own harvest <note>original: "Vintage"</note> at home. [The soul] is deprived of all peace, rest, contentment, and satisfaction in all its outward actions, whether in thought, word, or deed. But how does this happen? With this, the soul may kill and destroy all the essences <note>original: "Essences"; the fundamental nature or core qualities of a thing.</note> of the earthly life in a joined union with Christ. This sword is nothing other than the *power of the Spirit of Life, which is Christ’s deity in his eternal humanity*, now fixed in the center of the soul. It is Christ’s rising life within his saints, enabling them to conquer the essences of the earthly life (which is the **Beast**) <note>The "Beast" represents the fallen, carnal, or sinful nature of humanity that opposes the divine.</note> within themselves. Now, this taking away of peace from the earthly life is a blessed introduction to its final destruction, which is to follow according to the intention of the Kingly Redeemer.

The opening of the **third Seal** (Revelation 6:5–6).
</margin>

The opening of the **third Seal** shows Christ riding within us on a **black Horse**, with a pair of balances <note>original: "Ballances"; scales used for weighing.</note> in his hand. This signifies a famine brought upon the life of the "Beast" in his saints; it shows that the carnal self's day of plenty is passing away and that its earthly nature must be weighed in the balance of righteousness. For the life of the Lamb rises in his once-dead but now rising saints, putting them into one scale and weighing all their thoughts, words, and actions in the weight of perfection. Christ, the Holy One, is in the other scale to see whether they have been brought into an even poise <note>original: "Pois"; a state of balance or equilibrium.</note> with him. The goal is that all increase and plenty might richly abound in the fruitfulness of the Spirit. From this heavenly sowing, such a crop springs up that it proclaims a plenty exceeding all that came before. Thus, it will naturally spring up in the soul that is evenly balanced with Christ the Anointed. Nothing of the **oil and wine** <note>In a biblical context, oil and wine often represent the presence of the Holy Spirit and the joy of divine life.</note> which proceeds from this state shall ever be harmed or diminished.

<margin>
The opening of the **fourth Seal** (Revelation 6:7–8).
</margin>

At the opening of the **fourth Seal**, Christ rides within his saints on a powerful horse whose name is **Conquering Death**, and he has **Hell** following him. But though this Death looks grim, it is a death that shall only hurt the "fourth part of the earth"—that is, the evil principle in the earthly life. This principle reigns and holds dominion until the life of Christ is risen in his saints. Until that time, the **Beast** may be permitted to kill the life of Christ in the soul with hunger, famine, and a languishing <note>original: "languishing"; becoming weak or feeble.</note> death. For it is said that the Beast shall make war with the saints and for a time overcome them—that is, keep them suppressed by reigning over them.

As the express Word came to me, no one could or ever should come to the opening of the *seventh seal* until all this was fully finished. I must bear witness that it is currently happening, and it does not seem painful because of what is to succeed and follow when the next *seal* is opened.

<margin>The seven seals shown through a vision, some time after the opening of the other seals.</margin>
Now follows another great *mystery*, which I was called to examine, concerning the seventh seal. Within it lies hidden the reward and rich prize; although it has not yet been granted as a possession, the Holy One has seen it fit to reveal it by breathing forth this word within me, saying, *"Come aside and sanctify a fast, touching nothing of the leaven <note>In a biblical sense, leaven (yeast) often represents corruption or the pervasive influence of sin</note> found in the house of fallen nature. Abstain from every other cup except the one that I, the Anointed One, shall fill for you from the burning glassy sea. This will be a lamp to provide light and perfect knowledge of the most hidden things that everlasting Wisdom has until now kept sealed."* For to you who have reached the *sixth seal* <note>The stage of spiritual development involving intense inner purification and the "melting" of the old self</note>, and to your elements burning in the heat of that melting furnace, these further secrets are disclosed for your *consolation*—for those who endure in this final battle-race for conquest. Therefore, put on your *armor of life* day by day, and continue the fight for the joy of what is now revealed as your reward; take great care to avoid the leaven of human imagination, which may block the flow <note>original: "sluce" (sluice)</note> of pure revelation. The empty bowl began to fill with light, burning oil and became a rising spring, which gave light to the dark vessel <note>original: "socket"</note> of my inner understanding. The first thing shown to me was an open book, written on the inside, with every one of the *seven seals* broken. It was in the hand of the <term>God-man</term> <note>The glorified Christ, combining divine and human natures</note>, who said, *"Come and read the seven wonders that my faithful witness <note>John the Apostle, who recorded the Book of Revelation</note> spoke of in his day of Revelation, which were then to be sealed up because the day of their fulfillment was so far off."* But now, in this present time, there has been such a fervent heat of flaming intercession that all these seals have melted away, so that the secrets contained in the book might be opened and made known.

<margin>The seventh seal includes the seven thunders, which open as a result of this seal.</margin>
This *living book*, which was presented for me to read, was the glorified person of the Lord Jesus, who now appeared to reveal what was sealed in the *seven thunders* <note>Mysterious divine voices mentioned in Revelation 10:3–4, which John was originally commanded not to write down</note>. His presence was not terrifying, appearing as he did under the veil of pure humanity, where he hides me as if in the clefts of his own spiritual body, while

...while he talks with me; who said this: "*You have come to a deep knowledge; for here the greatest Good and highest Being stands unsealed to the inward-looking <note>original: "introverted"</note> eye of your spirit, which can enter into the light of eternity, where the mirror <note>original: "Looking-Glass"</note> of all mysteries stands open.*"

<margin>The first Thunder</margin> Now the cry of the first Thunder was this: "*Behold! See how the six-day creation has reached its limit, and Man ceases to be <note>This refers to the "old man" or the fallen nature of humanity</note>, having returned into the eternity of his own spirit to rest in God. This happens while the Number of the Beast is completed and his kingdom is finished, along with the laws <note>original: "Ordinances"</note> of day and night that exist in this changing world <note>original: "changeable Element"</note>. From this world, the craftiness and subtlety of human reason arise to support and maintain our fallen earthly life. None of this will be needed anymore, according to what the second Thunder declares.*"

<margin>The second Thunder.</margin> The second Thunder spoke as follows: "*Come and see those who now arise from the bed of passive stillness. They come forth entirely new and immortalized to rule like gods in an unchanging <note>original: "immutable"</note> creation. These are the ones who have been tested by great suffering and have lain as if dead under the seals. But they have come to life and take the Lamb's power to reign in God’s omnipotence—which is the only instrument they shall use to subdue everything, both inwardly and outwardly. For they are now commissioned under Him who has come to judge the living <note>original: "Quick"</note> and the dead, and to remove everything that has nourished the serpent's offspring <note>original: "Brood"</note>.*"

<margin>The third Thunder.</margin> The cry of the third Thunder is this: "*Come, you whose names are written in the Lamb’s Book of Life! The way is completely cleared, and the great River Euphrates is dried up to provide passage to the Holy Land. There, the Tree of Life shall meet you with its abundant fruits, whose flavor leaves a perpetual sweetness and a joy so strong it will make you realize that the Feast of Tabernacles <note>A biblical festival (Sukkot) symbolizing God dwelling with His people</note> has truly arrived to be celebrated with God alone. The walls are all lined with seraphim <note>High-ranking celestial beings or angels</note> appointed to guard you and usher you into a higher sphere and a more magnificent place than this—which was the home of the first man in Paradise—where you are* <gloss>to</gloss> *stay for only forty days. For a higher realm <note>original: "Orb"</note> descends like a cloud to take up the elders, who have their thrones prepared in the great city of Jerusalem. This is the permanent rest where no more displacement is feared; for there is an unbreakable <note>original: "indissoluable"</note> link, all fixed in a cooperative power that never closes.*"

<summary>This page details the messages of the first three "Thunders," which describe the end of the fallen earthly state, the resurrection of the faithful to rule with divine power, and the journey toward the New Jerusalem.</summary>

<margin>The fourth Thunder.</margin>

The fourth Thunder opened to me by way of a vision. I saw seven golden candlesticks branching out from a base <note>original: "Stud"</note> of gold, and each socket appeared empty. Then one appeared with the likeness of a jasper-light and poured in some sparkling liquid, and there arose a shining fire which sent forth numerous flakes of light. These ran up and down like lightning, so that nothing could contain them or prevent them from going wherever they wished. Then a voice cried out in my hearing: *"What have you seen? Nothing less than the great wonder of the fourth Thunder, revealing the fiery lamps that are appointed to be fixed in renewed human nature. These are so empowering that whoever they rest upon may act and do whatever they wish. For the harmony <note>original: "Concord"</note> of these seven spirits is of such force that they can save or destroy, bind or loose. They are the seven proper powers that must identify those who are God's co-deified <note>This term refers to humans who have been spiritually transformed to share in the divine nature</note> ones, commissioned to act in this last scene of wonders. This takes place in the Wonder-City, which shall be entirely composed of pure spirits. Hear and see: you shall be planted there as the great cannons; for these powers will multiply like a great army, which can cut off this visible world at a single stroke. This is the great reserve to be given as a reward to the suffering conquerors."*

<margin>The fifth Thunder.</margin>

The fifth Thunder cried: *"Now unseal the testimony of the Tabernacle; let the eternal Gospel of love and peace go forth in the cloven <note>divided or split, referencing the tongues of fire at Pentecost</note> tongue, which can only speak what springs from the original law of restored nature in its primary purity, out of the fountain of the Deity. Behold, these are the separated and anointed ones who have this Gospel dispensation <note>a divinely appointed system or period of time</note> as a perpetually rising spring proceeding from the Holy Spirit, who can indeed absolve and pronounce the remission of sins. For power will rest upon those who can convey the New Testament blood to heal and deliver others from the infectious root of sin. This will not be a fake <note>original: "painted"</note> or formal imitation; rather, a fire-breath shall go forth to send this healing Gospel into the very center and root of the sick and sorrowful in spirit, who have lain a long time waiting for a cure under the wound of sin. To this true royalty and priesthood, some shall be more eminently ordained for the gathering of those who lie under the Babylonian <note>symbolizing spiritual confusion or false religious systems</note> rubbish. They shall be raised through the outgoing might of the everlasting testimony in the blood of the Lamb's life, which is the only known character <note>an identifying mark or seal</note> that shall be given to them."*

<summary>The fourth and fifth thunders reveal the spiritual empowerment of "co-deified" believers and the proclamation of an eternal Gospel capable of healing the root of sin. These visions describe a new priesthood equipped with divine power to act as "cannons" against the visible world and to gather those trapped in spiritual confusion.</summary>

<margin>The sixth Thunder.</margin>
The *declaration* of the *sixth thunder* was shown to me in this way: I saw someone holding out a *sapphire signet* (or sealing *stone*), which was used to mark all the *free-born* citizens who belong to the *New Jerusalem*. For only those who are marked have the right to the vast *riches* concentrated in *Wisdom’s bank*. Until now, she has kept the *Bride's dowry* <note>In this context, the "Bride" is the Church or the body of the faithful, and the "dowry" represents spiritual gifts and inheritance.</note> locked away; she will not release it until the Bride comes with this *seal* upon her *heart*. This seal has now been brought forth to leave the ultimate mark of *sapphire-glory* and *purity*, signifying that the *Lamb’s Bride* is fully honored in this way. *Oh!* Who can now hold back these *riches, wealth*, and *honor* that have been stored up since before the *foundation* of the *world*? For everything must now be submitted to her command—whether in *heaven* or *earth*, in the glassy *sea*, in the *heights*, the *depths*, or any of the *eternal spheres*. The *name* on the seal is the *authority* everywhere; it must open everything that has been locked until now, because no one could bring this *seal* on their own. It has now been freely brought down, engraved by *God* himself, and therefore can never be forged; for it bears the living *image* <note>original: "Portraiture"</note> of the *Father, Son*, and *Spirit*, to whom all *riches, power*, and *glory* are given.

<margin>The seventh Thunder.</margin>
The last *thunder* arrives with great *power* to announce the *great day* of the *marriage feast* of the *Lamb*. The *fountains* now flow with that *spiritual wine* <note>original: "Spirit-Wine"</note> which existed within the *grape* before *time* began. Choice and precious *fruits*, ripened by the *serene* and *pure elements*, are prepared; and the *table* is covered with every *abundance* that *God* himself can provide to show the *contentment* and *joy* he feels on this final, fulfilling *day*. On this day, *Isaac* has brought his *Rebecca* <note>The author uses the biblical marriage of Isaac and Rebecca as an allegory for Christ (the Lamb) and the Church (the Bride).</note> home into the *glory* of *God the Father* and *Jerusalem*, where a perpetual *feast* of *joy* will be held forever from now on. *Angels*, sound your *trumpet songs* now, because the *harvest* is reaped and *God’s kingdom* has come. The *Lamb* and *Bride* have now met to sustain a living *legacy* <note>original: "Fame"</note> in a new *creation*. *Redemption* is finished, *salvation* is witnessed, and the *mystery* of *God* is unsealed. The *harpers* are summoned to the *holy choir* <note>original: "Quire"</note> with their *music* and fiery *tongues* to proclaim that the *Lamb* has handed over the *kingdom* (consisting of perfect *spirits*), so that *God* alone may be the crowning *majesty, strength, wisdom*, and *glory* in and over all the *inhabitants* of this *holy city* that bears his *name*. The *Almighty’s* creating *power* lives and makes its home here.

In this way, the seven thunders were opened and interpreted to me from the living Word <note>original: "springing Word"</note> for which I waited closely. I witnessed and felt a sweet and precious anointing and refreshing joy come from every cry of the thunders. Oh, this is so great, so significant, and so rich—such surprising goodness is found in this Book of Life, which is now being unsealed, that we should no longer focus on any other things.

Thus, the triumphant King and Conqueror (who possesses all these royal powers and honors that are folded within these seven thunders) has given his word. He promises that none of these shall fail in anyone who has the mark of the seventh conquering seal opened within themselves. Shared power and authority will be given to them to begin that reign which is to be established here on Earth, according to the prophecy in *John's Revelation*. This prophecy presses urgently upon me from the fiery cloud that circles my spirit like a defensive banner; it will let nothing of the overshadowing dark principle enter while the deep center of the soul’s fiery essence opens. Bright streams of light from the heart of the glorified Jesus meet me in such a peaceful, "love-paved" walk, where no one else can enter to see or hear what passes through vision or communication. However, I will faithfully share whatever I am ordered to declare, according to the mind of my Lord, who sees the effect that will follow all these revelations given by him.

Regarding the persons and place, much controversy has surrounded this mystery. I will not involve myself in that debate, but will only faithfully declare what the *Morning Star* <note>A title for Jesus Christ, derived from Revelation 22:16.</note> has expressly revealed from his own mouth, who began with this salutation: *“Awake, you who have been under death and judgment; do you not know that my reign is near? The preceding prophecy must be fulfilled, and there are those who are ripening for it, upon whom my kingdom shall be established. Therefore, observe the following details carefully, which are the preceding signs of my personal reign, by which it may be known how near it is.”*

The first detail concerns those people in whom this reign is to begin. These are people who have been "beheaded," suffering an inward spiritual martyrdom for the hope of a resurrection in a Godhead-body and spirit. The manner of death is to be noted here: they are said to have been beheaded. This means those who have had their "head-life"—where the rational understanding resides and from which all sensory operations originate—cut off and separated from them. This is because, through the rational mind, the *Dragon* and *Beast* <note>original: "Dragon and Beast" — symbolic figures representing evil and worldly power in the Book of Revelation.</note> have found a place to establish their kingdoms within humanity and have been sustained by it throughout all ages. The main strength of the *Serpent* has always resided in the craft and subtlety of reason, which is the "mark of the beast" upon the forehead.

Who then are those who have experienced this honorable death from the *flaming sword* in the hand of the *Cherubim* <note>In Genesis, the Cherubim guarded the way to the Tree of Life with a flaming sword; here, it symbolizes the spiritual process of destroying the ego or carnal reason.</note>, if not those who shall hear the resurrection trumpet? This trumpet lets them know their thrones are set, their conquering crowns are fitted to their new-risen heads, and that all power and judgment is given to them to represent the mighty King Jesus here on Earth. 

For Christ the Lord will have no need to come down in his own specific, glorified person among the inhabitants of the earth yet. There is something to be done first by those who are to represent him in kingly dominion. His transparent body is such that—until this first reign is finished—it would not be bearable to the current state of things, which must first be refined <note>original: "sublimed" — an alchemical term meaning to purify or elevate a substance to a higher spiritual state.</note>. It is enough that the Lord has come in his witnessing power and presence through a new-risen life with and in his elders, to reign in the majesty of his all-governing eye. This eye pierces like devouring flames and burning coals of fire, which run from...

<summary>The author explains that Christ’s "Personal Reign" begins within individuals who undergo a "spiritual beheading," meaning the sacrifice of their rational, worldly understanding to make room for divine rule. This internal transformation allows them to represent Christ’s authority on Earth before his literal, glorified physical return.</summary>

from one to another, and from one end of the Earth to the other. If the Lord will only supply our visible humanity with a mighty flow from the Godhead <note>original: "Deity"</note>, and with a witnessing omnipotence, it will be a sufficient and significant glory. 

Next, we must consider where the reign of Christ is to be manifested—namely, in the center of a renewed nature. This center is made entirely seraphic <note>original: "Seraphick" — like a seraph, the highest order of angels, characterized by fiery love and light.</note> in the soul's inward essence. From there, the **Serpent** and **Beast** <note>Symbolic figures from the Book of Revelation representing evil and worldly power.</note> are cast out and have no more place. In this final reign, they are chained up, even though during the Lord's previous dominion within the soul, he was always in conflict with these powerful adversaries. They exerted all their strength to keep their hold on the soul's mixed nature, but now that this structure has been overturned, they have no place to establish their rule. 

Thus, all strife ceases because the Lord alone reigns in peace over the entire region of light. The soul is now born anew into the world of light and is most firmly established in its kingly power. This power does not come from received gifts or abilities, such as those on the day of Pentecost, but instead, it all emerges from the "risen birth." <note>The spiritual rebirth into a divine state.</note> From this birth, powers flow naturally and unceasingly; therefore, they are beyond the danger of change or failure, which might otherwise threaten to disappoint anything agreed upon by the "kingly soul" and his seven counseling spirits. These spirits stand continually before the throne in the inward heavens. 

By the scattering coals from the Godhead, these heavens are made into an enduring, burning element where the glory of Christ’s person may be seen. However, he is not present in his individual person, but rather his likeness has risen through the power of the "creating Word" within his members. These members are entrusted with an active, powerful body suitable for managing such a kingdom. In this kingdom, the soul first reigns invisibly over all its own inward motivations and qualities—treating them as if they were people, nations, and languages. All of these are kept in a God-like balance <note>original: "temperature" — a harmonious mixture or state of being.</note> by Wisdom’s power. This demonstrates that the Lord has come to represent himself in a righteous and peaceful government, the likes of which have never been in the world since *Adam* lost Paradise.

*Objection.* But shall this be a universal reign? And in what manner shall it be manifested <note>original: "Modalized"</note> in this visible creation? That which is internal and spiritual will not be noticed much, even if it reaches this height of perfection, for it may be

<meta>This page continues the discussion of Christ's internal and external reign, specifically explaining that this transformation is a gradual process that begins with a small group of people rather than a universal event.</meta>

...hidden among a small number, yet they may be known only to one another, while the majority of people still remain under the Beast’s power and name, which is his accursed nature.

This ***Query*** <note>original: "Query" — a formal question or inquiry.</note> was answered for me in the plainness of truth, as follows: That this glorious Reign would not be universal, nor break out all at once, but would happen gradually through the burning anointing <note>original: "Unction" — a divine spiritual influence or anointing.</note> which prepares Christ’s way and makes room for his Kingdom. There is much to be done first, and here a "beheading" <note>In this context, the "beheading" refers to the spiritual death of the self-will or the rational mind, as described on the previous page.</note> must come before, along with a plucking up of everything that has caused desolation. After all this, there is a coming up again in the resurrection, which is a rare achievement indeed; for it is a resurrection to a state that is completely sound and sinless—the very same to which our Lord was raised after death. 

The prophecy does not say that all the dead were raised up to reign with Christ for a thousand years; instead, it is expressly declared that the rest of the dead did not rise until the thousand years had expired. From this, it was clarified for me that many may be going through this spiritual dying and yet remain dead while others are raised, according to the word: ***Blessed are they who are partakers of this first resurrection***; so, it will not be universal. 

Besides this, there are those who are unrighteous, of whom it is prophesied: ***Let him that is unjust, be unjust still***; these shall in no way enter into Christ’s Kingdom but will be shut out of the holy city, which shall rule over them. For this Kingdom shall not only be inward, within the properties of the soul, but shall also exercise its dominion over this visible realm <note>original: "Principle" — often used in mystical writing to refer to the physical or material world.</note>. Those who are unconverted shall be like stubble <note>Dry stalks left in a field after harvest, used here as a metaphor for something easily consumed by fire.</note> before the bright flaming cherubim, who shall reign on the earth in the Lamb’s power. 

The elect shall reap and long enjoy the creative works of their own hands; for the representatives of Christ the Lord shall be invested with no less power than that. This must be declared throughout all worlds: that Christ will appear in his saints who have risen again to bear the ***Trophy*** of a death-conquering power.

Now the next inquiry is: When is the time that this may be expected, because so far there is very little appearance of any such thing? That is, who is qualified to such a high degree as this heavenly Kingdom requires? Who are willing to be beheaded, and through death be made partakers of this reign?

<summary>The author explains that Christ’s millennial reign begins gradually within a small group of "beheaded" (self-sacrificed) believers rather than all at once. This "first resurrection" creates a sinless state where the elect rule over the visible world through divine power.</summary>

--- Page 108 ---
<meta>This page continues the discussion on the spiritual requirements for entering Christ’s Kingdom, shifting from the necessity of a spiritual "resurrection" to the specific internal obstacles—like fear and sensory reliance—that must be overcome before His reign can begin within the individual.</meta>

<page-num>28</page-num>

But despite all these objections raised by fear and unbelief, and the whole crowd of discouraging spirits belonging to the sensory life—whose cry is, *Who is able to endure all this?*—Faith and Love are granted to the worthy seekers <note>original: "pretenders" — in this context, it refers to those who aspire to or lay claim to the Kingdom, not those who are being fake.</note> of this Kingdom. By these virtues, they are carried as if on wings over all the floods of fear and discouragement. Those who are destined for it and whose names are found in the *Lamb’s List* <note>original: "Lamb's Roll" — a reference to the Book of Life in the biblical Book of Revelation.</note> shall not lack either the resolution or the courage to wade through the greatest opposing difficulties. The Lord has said he will give a double and triple portion of his Spirit to those who first abolish the "Law of Sense" <note>original: "Law of Sense" — the tendency to rely on physical feelings and worldly logic rather than spiritual intuition.</note>, which has been the primary hindrance to his Kingdom. This law must be nullified because it distracts the soul from drawing all its supplies from the *Rock* <note>original: "Rock" — a common biblical metaphor for Christ as a steady foundation.</note>. Furthermore, Faith cannot reach its full strength until the "Senses" are led into captivity and cast into a deep, "dead" sleep.

Now, regarding the time of the Lord’s Reign—when it shall take place—you will be inclined to say, *That this is an unknown thing, and that no one can determine the time in advance.* It is true that many people have calculated dates and troubled their spirits about it in vain. But if we want to know how near the time is, we must not look outside of ourselves, but into the unsealed *Book of Life* within us. There you will find the preceding signs, which must first be fulfilled in your own internal "Heavens and Earth." We may test and prove ourselves by what has already been revealed through the *seven seals* and *thunders* <note>Apocalyptic symbols from Revelation representing divine judgments and the unfolding of God's plan.</note>, to see how closely the Kingdom of our Lord has approached us. 

Let each of us examine ourselves to see how far this preparatory work has advanced within us. We can see what the kingdom of the *Beast* and the *Dragon* <note>Representations of the ego, sin, or worldly power that opposes the divine.</note> must suffer within us to make way for this Reign, which cannot be established until all this trash <note>original: "Trash" — the author's term for the sinful or worldly distractions that clutter the soul.</note> is cleared out of the way. Therefore, we shall only know the time by observing someone who delivers the first deadly blow and emerges as a Conqueror over the entire life of the senses and this outward kingdom, which currently stands divided between Good and Evil. My Jesus expressed that he would appear and embody a Kingly Reign within such an "Overcomer." We are not to look for any other sign at this time but the manifestation of this conquering life. He further said that an arrow would go forth from the *Word of Revelation*, which would stick so firmly in some people that it would pierce through the heart of
