...the Beast <note>The "Beast" is a common apocalyptic figure from the biblical Book of Revelation, representing worldly power or the unredeemed, carnal nature of humanity that opposes God.</note>, who has warred to hinder the Lord’s triumphant reign in his saints. From all this, it is clear that there is no surer sign of the approaching personal reign of Christ than to see the Beast wounded to the heart; for as soon as the Beast is removed, His Kingdom will appear—He whose right it is to rule in Heaven and Earth. Who will now absorb the essence <note>original: "suck in the Marrow"</note> of these precious things? This is the dripping oil distilling from the Olive Tree to make our way smooth, light, and clear, and to keep us from being daunted even if our lot <note>original: "Portion"</note> should be a drink <note>original: "draught"</note> of blood. For the life of the Beast must be taken away before the Lord appears within us as himself, in power, glory, and majesty. Oh, what attractions and desires these weighty sayings from the Lord may kindle in us for his coming to reign in absolute dominion within us!

The Word came to me: *Run hard; there is a rich prize <note>original: "Prey"</note> to be taken by treading down the Beast, which may be captured if pursued until victory. Therefore, be valiant in faith, for all power is in God to overcome whatever opposes his reign and personal Kingdom.*

<margin>January 2, 1682</margin>

I had much free communication with the Lord this night, and a cloud of light appeared within me, which raised my hope higher; I also had many questions satisfied. Then there appeared to me an upright banner <note>original: "pitched Standard"</note> that reached from the Earth to the Heavens, where its top was hidden. It resembled a straight, ascending flame, and it was indicated that this was the banner of overcoming power, which no earthly warrior would venture to engage with, for its dominion reached from the Earth to the Heavens. This word was added: *Stick close to it, and do not move from under it, for it will be a shelter from the storm that is coming, along with a flowing sea of troubles raised by the wrathful one <note>A reference to the devil or the spirit of antichrist.</note>, whose kingdom is division.*

This motto and inscription was given from the sealed book within, as follows: *In Christ I live, in Christ I die, so that I may cease from sin and animalistic nature <note>original: "beastly Properties"</note>, so that the first ground of life may rise over all to make the reign of Christ immortal.* Words shall no longer be like empty sails spread out, but shall become the thundering breath <note>original: "Air"</note> of an almighty power—which is Christ in his essential Spirit—who will, in his own time, show wonders <note>original: "wanders"</note> from the heights above and the depths below.

But despite all these objections of fear and unbelief, and the whole crowd of discouraging spirits belonging to the life of the senses <note>original: "sensible Life"; referring to a life governed by physical feelings and worldly perceptions rather than the spirit.</note>, whose cry is, "Who is able to suffer all this?" Faith and Love are given to the worthy seekers <note>original: "pretenders"</note> of this Kingdom. By these, they are carried as if on wings over all the floods of fear and discouragement. Nor shall those who are destined for it and found in the Lamb’s Book of Life <note>original: "Lamb's Roll"</note> lack either the resolution or the courage to wade through the greatest opposing difficulties. The Lord has said he will add a double and triple portion of his Spirit to those who shall first abolish <note>original: "abrogate"</note> the "Law of the Senses." This law has been the chief hindrance to his Kingdom and must be made void because it distracts the soul from drawing all its supplies from the Rock <note>A biblical reference to God or Christ as the ultimate source of spiritual sustenance.</note>. Neither can Faith arrive at its full strength until the senses are led into captivity and cast into a deep sleep.

Now, as to the time of the Lord’s reign and when it shall be, you will be apt to say, *“This is an unknown thing, and no one can set a date for it.”* It is true that many have made calculations and puzzled their spirits about it in vain. But if we want to know how near the time is, we must not look outside ourselves, but into the unsealed Book of Life within us. There you will find the preceding signs, which must first be accomplished in your own inner "heavens and earth." We may test and prove ourselves by what has already been revealed through the *seven seals* and *thunders* <note>Apocalyptic symbols from the biblical Book of Revelation, here interpreted as internal spiritual milestones or trials.</note> to see how near the Kingdom of our Lord has approached us.

Therefore, we shall only know the time by such a person who shall deliver the first deadly blow and emerge as a conqueror over the entire life of the senses and this outward kingdom, which stands divided between good and evil. <margin>January 2nd, 1682.</margin>
I had much free communication with the Lord this night, and a cloud of light appeared within me, which raised my hope higher; I also had many questions satisfied. This power is Christ in his essential Spirit, who will show wonders from the heights above and the depths below in his own time.

<page-num>30</page-num>

## Look and read the circle line, and you may further know and find the most marvelous things.

Upon some inward conversation with my Lord about the shipwrecked vessel <note>original: "Ship-wracked-Bark"</note> of nature, which still lies upon a foreign coast and is often in jeopardy because of certain tides that might make the vessel spring a leak, the Lord immediately entered with these words, saying, *"Reflect and ponder well upon my grace and love, which has been so great as to join my life so deeply with yours, that you can suffer nothing without me calling it my own."* Your broken vessel shall be restored to its original state when all sinful qualities are drowned in the Alpha and Omega <note>The first and last letters of the Greek alphabet, representing Christ as the beginning and the end (Revelation 22:13).</note>, who is the beginning and end of time. He will open the sealed mine where unsearchable riches shall freely abound, as in the state of innocence where no necessity or want was known. *All of this is progressing and advancing in the moving circle of the Deity; all that is required of you is to remain steady in the highest flow of faith.*

<margin>As soon as desired; this should be understood as being carried up in a spiritual transport <note>original: "Spiritual Transportion"; an ecstatic or out-of-body spiritual experience.</note>.</margin>
My spirit was lowered into the deep center and circumference, where the Personal Glory met me. I had great happiness in beholding the Shepherd with his flock, who greeted me in a friendly way. For although I was a naked spirit, I was not despised by such dignified beings, though I was made very aware of my lack of such a glorious body. As soon as I desired it, I was immediately embodied in that same glory and joined to the heavenly procession <note>original: "Train".</note>. I rejoiced greatly that I had slipped out of the vile garment <note>The physical body, often described as "vile" in comparison to the spiritual body in 17th-century mystical writing.</note>, even if only for a while, and although I had to put it on again and appear among the inhabitants of time until my journey was fulfilled. 

This bright clothing can only be worn by the natives of Jerusalem <note>Refers to the "New Jerusalem" or the citizens of heaven.</note>; and although these were the most desirable flocks with whom I would choose to remain forever, I found a prohibition for the present; it must not yet be desired. This royal priestly garment is reserved until we are called into the presence and have communion with the most Holy of Beings, who did not bring me here to send me away empty-handed, but gave me a taste of the choice fruits of that unknown climate. The refreshing taste of these fruits remains upon the center-mouth of my heart, from which the fruits of life should spring forevermore. This was said to me with a notice <note>original: "Advertisement"; here meaning a piece of advice or formal notification.</note> to wait until Wisdom’s garden is planted in the soul and richly furnished with those blossoming

<summary>The author describes a divine promise to restore "shipwrecked" human nature and recounts a mystical vision of being temporarily clothed in a heavenly body among the residents of the New Jerusalem.</summary>

...powers that may draw the high and lofty One to look down and walk there.

Oh! What are these powerful forces <note>original: "forcible Powers"</note> that I feel winding my spirit deeper within? I asked what the sign would be for those who dwell in the center <note>original: "Center-dwellers"</note> of the most holy of Beings—a sign which would need no witness from man, for human witness is poor and insufficient. Therefore, be quiet and still. Cease from seeking all evidence from created things <note>original: "Creaturely Evidence"</note>, and look instead for the witness of the unsealed book. This book will exercise the seven flowing properties of the Holy Spirit, which will outlast all the spoken testimonies that are so common <note>original: "fluent"</note> in this age. 

Because of this, a more certain seal is provided that will remove all doubts and suspicions. I was called to drink from this festive cup, given by the hand of him who is appointed to pour it out. This cup is the final and great reserve that shall silence all controversies and doubtful arguments. It will ratify and confirm those who are born again to act, move, and live from a new, generating spring—a spring that washes away everything that defiles or causes obstruction. These seven evidencing powers were revealed to me, and they are what the personal reign of Christ will consist of.

<margin>First Property.</margin>
The first was a circulating cloud of light. From this cloud, constant revelation would spring and reveal what was never known before concerning the invisible scenes of glory. Mortal man has been a complete stranger to these things, not having understood his original royalty. But where Christ, the central light, has entered into the soul’s essence, an everlasting light breaks forth. This light shows invisible things in their first pure ground, where the eternal fire-soul <note>The "fire-soul" likely refers to the core of the human spirit or the "spark" of divine life within a person.</note> moves strongly to escape an impotent, weak life that has been shut up in this vile body of sin. The light of revelation makes the soul restless to stay there any longer. Thus, it is driven to pursue a new beginning from a newly rising spring that flows from the Godhead. This spring provides strength for a "co-deified" operation <note>original: "Co-deified operation"; a state where the human soul acts in union with the divine nature.</note>, which may give a living testimony that a new spring is appearing out of an old tree. The tree's sap is renewed to bring forth the second property of the Holy Spirit, like another fruit-bearing branch.

<margin>Second Property.</margin>
This branch is put forth from the all-encompassing element in which the soul is baptized, as if in a cloud of fire. Whoever they may be...

Those who come to know this need no greater witness for their own part, for they know their dwelling is in the very heart and center of God. They are fed and cherished with the Blood of Life and an anointed lamp <note>original: "oily Lamp"; the oil represents the nourishing presence of the Holy Spirit</note> that causes the entire inner body to be like a burning Cherub, which at certain times and seasons can mount and fly into the very Heavens. Though this is still a mystical and obscure way of speaking, and little experienced by people yet, know that the time of Elijah is near. A whirlwind is descending from the Alpha and Omega to take up the "slain witnesses" <note>a reference to the martyred prophets in Revelation 11:7-12</note> who have been so undervalued by the world.

The Gate of Ascension is unlocked by this bright Key, allowing one to follow Him who was translated from among men so as to be hidden in the Mystery. And it shall happen this way again, though in a different form and model. For these Elijah-prophets, though they shall be redeemed from everything belonging to a degenerate nature, they shall differ from Elijah in this: whereas he was taken up and seen no more among men, these shall remain on the earth in their visible persons. Everywhere they go, they shall carry with them their surrounding "chariots of fire," which will be visible to none but the "ministering Elijahs" who will not cease to follow closely wherever the witness of life remains in this manner and kind.

<margin>Third Property</margin>
The third outflowing property of the Holy Spirit, which ushers in Christ’s reign, is this: a simple and naked soul, emerging from the baptizing cloud, puts on a garment of invincible defense and power—namely, a purely magical body <note>original: "Magical Body"; in this mystical context, "magical" refers to a body transformed by divine power into a supernatural, spiritualized state</note>. This is the same kind of body the Lord Christ had after his Resurrection, consisting of the one pure element that can swallow up the visible, coarse substance of the flesh, so that it may no longer be a hindrance to the soul running its race.

Indeed, such a body is very suitable for the great things that are to be accomplished by the "Christed" kings on earth. If Christ did not provide such a body, it would be useless for him to attempt to establish his kingdom where the Beast and the Dragon <note>referring to the antagonistic powers in the Book of Revelation</note> have held dominion for so long, and who will not be dethroned easily. They will try to keep their hold on the outward shell of the body when they can no longer find a place for themselves in the soul. Therefore, the Lord has revealed that he will turn the body itself back into its paradisaical state <note>original: "Paradifical Property"; the state of the human body as it existed in the Garden of Eden before the Fall</note> again, so that the prince of darkness and his...

...on a new nature. Hostile forces <note>original: "Agents"</note> may not be able to overthrow the Lord’s personal reign in his Saints; they would desire no easier target to shoot their arrows at than a frail, mortal body of flesh. Therefore the Lord has said that he will raise a firm tabernacle <note>a spiritual dwelling or body</note> which no earthly weapon shall be able to pierce, and which will be a suitable garment to last throughout the thousand years. Let the just and holy believe this, and see all this finished first in the kingdom that is within the soul’s essence. Then, such people may conclude that they shall assume a visible power and right to reign with Christ over all kingdoms of this world in magical might <note>original: "Magical Might"; in this context, "magical" refers to divine, supernatural power acting directly upon the physical world</note>.

<margin>Fourth Property.</margin>
The *fourth* outgoing power of the Holy Ghost sets the soul free in eternal liberty from all conflicts which the *Dragon* <note>a common biblical name for Satan</note> or the "starry region" <note>the influence of fate or astrology</note> has introduced. For the soul is now influenced by those superior planets to which these outward planets are subject. The *soul* and *Christ* are in a mutual agreement to break away from the infectious rays of time and mortality. They are encompassed by that Sun that will set no more, nor withdraw; for it is the "glory of seven days" <note>see Isaiah 30:26, referring to a time of perfect divine light</note> that runs its circle without the least diminishing of its light, and fixes the soul in its pure *radiant body* <note>original: "splendorious Body"</note>. 

Then the soul shall find itself in another form of life, seated as a high principality before *Jehovah’s* throne, attending there to know the counsel and decrees of the Sovereign *Majesty*. Under this Majesty, all inferior government is managed, which shall be committed to those who reign with Christ the Lord. Oh! This will be a wonderful time indeed! For while the Lord is opening these things to me, I am like one sunk into the *abyssal ground* <note>a mystical term for the deep, bottomless center of God's presence</note>, where I am as nothing, until he puts forth a piercing ray from his flaming eye to fetch my spirit up again. He spoke to me in this way: *“Stand up, and hear and declare the new model of my reign, and let it be published; for it is time to awaken those who are ordained for the first resurrection <note>see Revelation 20:5-6, referring to the righteous who are raised to life at the start of the Millennium</note>, so that they may no longer sleep in the daybreak of my appearance. And do you solemnly set your heart to the great privileges <note>original: "Immunities"</note> that are revealed—not only for speculation, but for fulfillment, where great earnestness shall be found.”* 

Thus the Holy One brought home his word, and caused it to pass through a vessel that is of no account. I myself stand amazed that the Lord would choose to appear and hold such free conversation, and leave such weighty things...

<margin>Fifth Property.</margin>
The fifth property of the Holy Spirit <note>original: "Holy Ghost"</note> is that by which the soul gets free from the conflicting elements and arrives at rest and peace, as all wars and battles cease. Having climbed these four ascending steps toward the throne, the *soul* can now look down like a tower of strength, intimidating to all the lower inhabitants nearby. It is a palace complete within itself, knowing nothing but serene peace and harmony. Neither the *dragon* nor the *beast* <note>biblical symbols for the devil or worldly evil</note> even attempt to come here, because love and peace are their version of hell and torment. Christ’s personal reign within his saints consists of *peace, power, love, and joy*. The Holy Spirit moves in a mild and gentle way, flowing from the *water of life*, which overcomes <note>original: "captivates"</note> enmity and strife and seals away the dark center along with all its spirits. This has a two-fold meaning: first, it refers to the love and peace that fill the entire internal *hemisphere* <note>the inner world or spiritual domain</note> of the soul; and second, it refers to that same love flowing out and spreading through all the subjects of that peaceful kingdom which Christ will establish in the high *angelic harmony of love*.

<margin>Sixth Property of the soul.</margin>
The sixth outgoing property is the omnipotence of God exerted within the renewed part of the person, in an absolute and almighty dominion that allows for no opposition <note>original: "controule"</note>. Here, there is no need to look elsewhere to borrow gifts or powers, for they are now firmly established in the soul to manifest the wonders of the *magia* <note>original: "Magia"; divine or supernatural power through which spiritual reality acts upon the physical world</note>. This power had been hidden within the "principle of wisdom," whose virgin crown of power is now sent down as a clear sign of the kingly reign that must prevail over all the earth. The bright, flaming standard of *Christ’s personality* <note>referring to the actual presence or personhood of Christ manifested in the believer</note> will be so magnificent that it will draw home the dispersed and scattered flock that has suffered violence in this "Babylonish" kingdom <note>referring to the corrupt, confused, and materialistic state of the world</note>. A *jubilee* <note>original: "Jubile"; a season of liberation, restoration, and the canceling of debts</note> will be proclaimed by those to whom this dominion first arrives. They, the head-kings and leaders, are appointed to shine like stars; they will hold such influence through God’s omnipotence that all the elect <note>those chosen by God for salvation or leadership</note> will hurry and fly to this kingdom like *doves to the windows* <note>a reference to Isaiah 60:8, symbolizing a peaceful and eager return to God</note>. There, all reasons for complaining about the necessities of life will be eliminated, because the storehouse of wisdom shall from

the *inherent omnipotence* <note>original: "Inherent Omnipotency"; the quality of having all power within oneself</note> will abundantly supply everything that is needed of this kind. The lost power of *Paradise* <note>original: "Paradifical"</note> shall be restored to a higher degree than ever before, so that *self-love* and *selfish possessiveness* <note>original: "Propriety"; here meaning the sense of exclusive ownership or self-interest as opposed to communal divine love</note> may be taken away. These cannot enter into Christ’s kingdom; for the Lord has declared that no one with such a spirit shall ever inherit his kingdom. The vast and inexhaustible treasure of divine riches cannot be enjoyed as *private property*, but only through *love’s communion*.

<margin>The Seventh Property.</margin>
The *seventh* and last property of the Holy Spirit <note>original: "holy Ghost"</note>, which testifies to *Christ’s reign*, consists of a life of *praise and exaltation*. This praise is expressed through *angelic tongues, powers, and sounds*, like many perfumed offerings. It pays an everlasting *tribute of love* and joy to the *Ancient of Days* <note>a biblical title for God, specifically from the Book of Daniel</note>, by and in whom the saints have come to reign over all kingdoms in this world, through Christ’s personal power. The *new hallelujahs* arising from the *fiery breath* of the *Holy Spirit* shall be the *holy activity* <note>original: "Holy Sport"</note> and *celestial play* of the children of this kingdom. All their works shall proclaim the praises of the Most High; for the spirit of *Wisdom* <note>often referring to Sophia, the personification of Divine Wisdom</note> will act with such loftiness and in such a mysterious manner as has never been known until now. All these miraculous powers have been reserved to complete the glory of Christ’s second coming through his representatives here on earth.

For if the Lord did not demonstrate his *divine power* <note>original: "God-head-Power"</note> in his saints in this way, they would be held in very low esteem; it would be a pointless thing to assume the *title* and *name of kings* without an anointing power suitable for it. No dominion, whether internal or external, can be maintained without the *omnipotence of love*. And truly, my Lord has assured me that he has made large and full provision for this day. In response to some of my own private objections, he said to me: *Do not be at all doubtful concerning all the great things of my kingdom which have been revealed to you from the center of light; they have been revealed as a foundation for faith. The hand of the clock* <note>original: "Head of the Dyal"</note> *is moving toward the appointed hour which will reveal all these working powers of the Holy Spirit. Therefore, do not limit yourself, nor anyone else to whom this word shall come; but expand into a vast and spacious belief, matching the power of love and the greatness of the Lord, toward whom the eye of faith is directed. For wonderful things are in motion* <note>original: "upon the wheel"</note>, *by which you may know it is high time to reach out your hands to raise and awaken that*

<page-num>(36)</page-num>

...which has been so long asleep. This was the concluding word of counsel from the Lord.

The previously mentioned properties <note>original: "fore-going Properties"</note> are what make way for the reign of Christ in personal power and glory. This is now the great event to be waited for, as the end and fulfillment <note>original: "Consummation"</note> of everything that has been revealed. We are not to rest simply in having discovered this, but we are to press forward to enjoy the fruit or result of it in the inward center of our being, where Christ will be manifested in his personal glory and power. 

For the driving, seeking spirit will not be at rest until it can put on its resurrection body. It knows that up to this point—despite all its refinement and the dying of its sinful self and the world—it still remains a naked spirit. It must return into the virgin womb of eternity to be reborn into a heavenly body <note>original: "Corporeity"</note> made from the one holy element from which Christ’s own holy personhood is formed. This is the rich garment with which the kings of Zion <note>original: "Sion-Kings"</note> are to be clothed. By this, they will be distinguished from those who remain stuck in their corrupted nature and are not reborn into this bright image. They will also be distinguished from those who, although they are reborn in their inner being <note>original: "inward man"</note>, are still entangled in the distractions of many worldly things. These people are forced <note>original: "fain"</note> to stay behind until those who have overcome the world, the beast, and the dragon <note>figures from the Book of Revelation representing worldly and demonic opposition to God</note> by pressing forward have succeeded. 

Therefore, be among the first to be established <note>original: "instated"</note> in the Lord’s personal power. The Lord assured me that in this present age, he would establish some who are known to him in his personal reign. They will enjoy their first heavens here on earth, much to the terror, grief, and envy of those who have been harsh and cruel in the kingdom of the beast, withholding their goods from the lambs of Christ’s flock. 

In this state, it will be like the story of ***Dives*** and ***Lazarus*** <note>a biblical parable in Luke 16 where a poor man, Lazarus, is honored in heaven while a rich man, Dives, is punished</note>. The oppressors will see those they once despised being greatly beloved and honored. These humble ones will be entrusted with a kingdom in which they shall reign as true-born co-heirs with their head, Christ, ruling over all those who were their oppressors in this world. It was also revealed to me that there were others in a mixed state, divided between the two kingdoms of light and darkness, but who still pursued the goal <note>original: "Mark"</note> and the crown. These people would hardly believe...

...which has been so long asleep. This was the concluding word of counsel from the Lord.

The previously mentioned properties <note>original: "fore-going Properties"</note> are what make way for the reign of Christ in personal power and glory. This is now the great event to be waited for, as the end and fulfillment <note>original: "Consummation"</note> of everything that has been revealed. We are not to rest simply in having discovered this, but we must press forward to enjoy the fruit or result of it in our inner center, where Christ will be manifested in his personal glory and power. 

For the active, seeking spirit will not be at rest until it can put on its resurrection body. It knows that up to this point—notwithstanding all its refinement and the dying of its sinful self and its attachment to the world—it still remains a naked spirit. It must return into the "virgin womb of eternity" to be regenerated into a heavenly physical form <note>original: "Corporeity"</note> made from the one holy element from which Christ’s own holy personhood is formed. 

This is the rich garment with which the kings of Zion <note>original: "Sion-Kings"</note> are to be clothed. By this, they will be distinguished from those who remain stuck in their corrupted nature and are not reborn into this bright image. They will also be distinguished from those who, although they are reborn in their inner being <note>original: "inward man"</note>, are still entangled in the distractions of many worldly things. These people are forced <note>original: "fain"</note> to stay behind until those who—by pressing forward hard—have overcome the *world*, the *beast*, and the *dragon* <note>In the Book of Revelation, these figures represent worldly systems and spiritual forces opposed to God</note>. 

Therefore, let these be first established in the Lord’s personal power. For the Lord assured me that this would be the way he would take in this present age: to establish some who are known to him in his personal reign, so that they may enjoy their "first heavens" here on earth. This will be to the terror, grief, and envy of those who have been hard and cruel in the kingdom of the beast, withholding good things from the lambs of Christ’s flock. 

Here it will be as in the case of *Dives* and *Lazarus* <note>A biblical parable from Luke 16:19-31 where the roles of a rich man (traditionally called Dives) and a poor beggar (Lazarus) are reversed in the afterlife</note>. They shall see those who were most despicable in their eyes being greatly beloved and honored. These faithful ones will be entrusted with a kingdom in which they shall reign as true-born co-heirs with their head, Christ, ruling over all those who have been their oppressors in this world. 

It was also revealed to me that there were others who stood in a mixed state, divided between the two kingdoms of light and darkness, but who nevertheless followed after the goal <note>original: "Mark"</note> and the crown. These would scarcely be—

...believe that such a commission as this would ever be given to anyone appearing in a mortal form—to be crowned as co-heirs with the Lord in his personal glory. But the Just One has shown that it shall be this way, in order to convince and provoke those last mentioned to envy <note>original: "Emulation"</note>, so that they too might hurry to be made kings and priests to God.

The grand question <note>original: "Query"</note> after all that has been revealed concerning the reign of Christ in his saints is this: *Where will this government be established?* It is an ancient scriptural prophecy that *Judah* shall reign with God, for he is faithful with the saints. The scepter shall not depart until *Shiloh* comes—he who is the King and Lawgiver, who is, was, and is to come. 

And to whom will he come first, if not to those pure virgin-spirits who labor <note>original: "travel," meaning travail or labor in childbirth</note> to bring forth Christ spiritually? This is the product of an invisible Almightiness, which will appear as that "Man-Child" who is to rule the nations with the rod of God’s power in this world. He will perform far greater wonders with it than *Moses* ever did with his. 

For the eternal divine creativity <note>original: "Magia," referring to the supernatural power of God's wisdom to manifest reality</note> shall bring forth all her famous plants. The highest *Wisdom* shall be brought into manifestation and replenish the earth anew, so that God may walk in the midst of it and no longer be unknown to its inhabitants. These inhabitants have been refined from their waste <note>original: "Dross"</note> and have thereby gained a free and open entrance into the *Jerusalem* from above. The partition wall must be broken down, so that the Christ above and the Christ beneath may join together for mutual joy and fulfillment <note>original: "Fruition"</note>.

But here someone might object: *How can this be as long as the body is still earthly <note>original: "Terrestrial"</note>? For an earthly body cannot ascend to the Lord’s glory.*

I answer: For this reason, a new body is prepared—even a heavenly one—like the one Christ had, who could change his form however and whenever he pleased. Thus, he appeared to *Moses* and *Elijah* <note>original: "Elias"</note> in his bright, transfigured body, and then came down again in an earthly body into the sight of mortals. This power shall be renewed in those who will represent <note>original: "personate"</note> Christ upon the earth. They shall ascend and descend at will and transform <note>original: "transmute"</note> themselves easily. This state will far excel all visions of the glorified person of Christ, for without this celestial <note>original: "Cœlestial"</note> body, we cannot endure that sublime and angelic <note>original: "Seraphick"</note> communion...

<margin>Revelation 12:1</margin>
The sum of all these discoveries concerning the Reign of *Christ* must culminate <note>original: "be terminated"</note> in the Virgin seed. *Eve's* lost chastity will produce a new Generation, to whom this Kingdom shall be committed in trust. This was revealed from that Vision which Holy *John* saw of a great wonder in Heaven: *a Woman clothed with the Sun, and the Moon under her feet, and a Crown of twelve Stars on her head.* Many interpretations have been made of this Vision, which I will neither approve of nor condemn here; rather, I will focus on the opening of the living Word, who is present to explain whatever is obscure to those who turn away from the wisdom of the flesh and receive the united drops as they fall from the Heavenly Cloud. To receive these, a well-purified vessel is required, in which no taint of decay <note>original: "putrifaction"</note> remains.

This was a condition <note>original: "Proviso"</note> given to me to observe throughout all this ministry <note>original: "Ministration"</note>, which made me set time apart to prepare the vessel before I could see the Cloud break and send down its shower upon my ground. I mention this for the sake of those who are also in this ministry of Revelation.

Now to proceed to what the Lord has opened to me from this figure of the Woman, who is represented here as laboring <note>original: "travelling," meaning travailing in childbirth</note> in Birth. This suggests to us that just as a sinful offspring was brought forth by a woman—in which Sorrow, Sin, and the Curse had dominion—so from this Eternal Virgin-Wisdom, a birth is to be born in which nothing but Joy, Life, Blessing, and Eternal Power and Dominion shall take place.

This Virgin nature *Adam* had within himself before *Eve* was ever taken out of him. But she withdrew as soon as *Adam* looked outward, as if he were not sufficient of himself to increase and multiply for the replenishing of Paradise, God having created him Male and Female within himself. But this design was overturned, and *Eve's* Generation is now multiplied over the face of the whole Earth, from which Paradise and the Virgin have fled; yet a wonder

<margin>Meaning Wisdom, who is God's espoused Virgin.</margin>
This last point indeed may be considered a wonder beyond our hopes and expectations, which she is nevertheless ready to do. This is well known to those who have understood her and preferred her above all things, being well assured that just as all Bliss and Peace fled away with her, so upon her return, all good will come back with her again. As for her Nativity <note>Her origin or birth.</note>, it is from everlasting, before the Creation of Heaven or Earth. She was before all things, being the Co-essential creating Power in the Deity, which formed all things out of nothing. She has given a dignified existence to all those Seraphic Glories <note>Angelic beings of the highest order.</note> which move within her own sphere; they are the product of an unsearchable Wisdom, meant for the replenishing of those superior Worlds which are little known in this world, where we are outcasts. Neither can we be acquainted with such glories until we are born again out of her Virgin-Womb; for it must be so before we can ever become like Christ <note>original: "to be Christs"</note>, in conformity with Christ our Head.

A marvelous thing the Lord has revealed to me concerning this Woman whom holy John saw <note>A reference to the vision in Revelation 12.</note>: namely, that she represents God’s Spouse and Mate, from whom the highest Birth—the Eternal Word of Wisdom—went forth as the mighty First and Last <note>original: "Alpha and Omega"</note>, in a distinct glorious personality known to himself in God and the Everlasting Virgin, before any of the heavenly hosts existed.

In alchemy and mysticism, the "quinta essentia" or fifth essence is the purest, most incorruptible part of a substance.</note> will again be found, which will give a most glorious luster and renew all gross, vile matter, acting as the Wisdom to show forth God’s wonders; all of which are reserved until her day. But it may be queried, *How can this heavenly Virgin endure to come down upon this defiled Earth to multiply her offspring in it? For without doubt the Serpent and Dragon will violently rage against her, which we have experience of in ourselves, when he crowds into our very Heavens, with all evil Beasts at his Heels, waging War against us.*

Yet nothing of all this shall be able to prevent God’s purpose, who will appear with his *Virgin* in the divine Body, and cover her with the **one element** <note>In this context, the "one element" refers to a pure, spiritual substance that transcends the four fallen elements of the physical world.</note>, which will torment all...

...evil spirits, and drive them away <note>original: "wear them out." Here meaning to harass or exhaust the influence of evil spirits until they depart.</note>, for they can no more endure her virgin purity than the virgin spirit can bear their poison. Therefore, a separation is made, as we read: *the Woman had a place prepared for her in the wilderness*. This signifies the *Spirit of Faith*, which carries the soul away from the sensual ground where a crowd of fluttering spirits of all kinds gather. Therefore, this is no place for God’s Virgin to stay; she might be in danger of having her chastity violated or being drowned by the wrathful floods of the Dragon. 

The Serpent will not cease to make his attempts with more subtlety than ever, because his power <note>original: "Head-power." A reference to Genesis 3:15, where the seed of the woman crushes the serpent's head.</note> shall be crushed and his offspring disinherited by this newly introduced *Virgin-Spirit*. But the *Virgin-Spirit* shall mount like an eagle and so escape the Dragon’s flood. A fountain is opened in the desert, and the soul in union with the Virgin shall drink from it instead of the muddy waters of the earth, which she loathes. Pleasant food is provided in this wilderness by God himself and his Christ. 

The Lord provides these delicacies to the "abstracted" life <note>original: "abstructed Life." In this context, a life withdrawn from worldly distractions and focused entirely on the spiritual.</note>, where the *Virgin-Spirit* is a stranger to the sensory-driven self within, as well as to the enchanting world without. Here, she lacks no good company, for she is never without heavenly visitors; she reserves herself chiefly for conversation with the Deity, from whom she expects all her blessing and growth. Thus, the soul shall flourish and multiply where the *Virgin* has made herself real. Her wilderness shall become a rich *Sharon pasture* <note>original: "Sharon-Pasture." Refers to the Plain of Sharon, a region in Israel famous in the Bible for its fertility and beauty.</note>, a land of unknown plenty, where gold, silver, and precious stones shall be as common as the dust under the feet of these holy, set-apart virgins. Also, both inwardly and outwardly, they shall feed on power, joy, and peace until the "division of time" <note>original: "diviſion of Time." A prophetic term, likely referring to the "time, times, and half a time" in the Book of Revelation, marking the end of an era.</note> comes to an end, at which point a further mystery will be understood. But oh, let all this first be fully witnessed!

Here follows a further announcement from Him who has come to fulfill old prophecies in the substance of his own person—He who is born of the Virgin. From Him, we may expect the perfecting of everything that all previous ministries and revelations have left incomplete, regarding those wonders which shall be performed in this final age. To this end, Christ has opened the <term>Center-Eye</term> <note>original: "Center-Eye." Often used in mystical writing to describe an "inner eye" or spiritual perception that sees into the divine essence.</note>, which can pierce as deep as the Godhead itself, where this Virgin lies hidden...

...with all her glory, who has been obscured ever since Adam’s day because she would not trust herself to any mortal. Only some of her shining rays have been scattered now and then into holy souls. A taste of this has been given as the "first fruits" of what is reserved for Christ the Lord’s second birthday, when a *Pentecost-Feast* shall be provided according to the royal and heavenly state of *Jehovah’s* Bride and Mate. 

Oh, how unable am I to express in words what has been shown to me concerning the later fruits that *Wisdom’s Children* shall feed upon in that day! We will pass by that discovery for now and first define who this wonderful Virgin is and the nobility of her origin <note>original: "Extract." Meaning her lineage or spiritual essence.</note>. She is taken into God’s bosom in a mutual embrace of love, and therefore may be termed the *eternal Goddess* in a high and serious sense; it is an evil and sinful thing to give this title to any other, as is too often done. 

But let us proceed to describe this *Virgin* according to the depiction God has given of her. First, as to her matter and substance: it is of such thin, pure, and airy subtlety that nothing of the gross and heavy outward elements can mix with it. They would only tarnish her clarity <note>original: "Clarity." Here meaning her luminous purity or brightness.</note>, which is always in communion with the Deity. This was symbolized by the *Sun* and *Stars* seen around the head of the *Woman* in the *Book of Revelation*, signifying those superior *planets* <note>In the mystical astrology of this period, "superior planets" represented higher spiritual influences that governed the soul, as opposed to "inferior" ones that governed the physical body.</note> which cannot be corrupted by the defilements of this inferior world <note>original: "Orb."</note>, as they possess far higher and more exalted powers and hold dominion over all that is beneath them. 

For just as the lower *planets* keep the soul in subjection to the Curse, so these superior ones deliver and set it free. Therefore, it is entirely worth asking: in what *planet* may the nativity of the *Virgin* be renewed? This must be through bright *Venus* <note>original: "Venus." Often used in this context to represent divine love.</note>, who must bring forth the mighty birth under *Sol* <note>original: "Sol." Latin for the Sun, representing the Christ-light.</note>, in conjunction with the rest of the superior *planets* which are all harmonized as one. For they agree to bring forth the *Mother*, and then the *Son*, in the *Virgin-Humanity*. This humanity takes both its figure and form *organically* <note>meaning "functioning as a living instrument."</note> from heavenly matter to operate in various properties, which are described by the highest *Wisdom* to illustrate the only true *Virgin* in her beautiful form. The divine mold for her shape is found again, matching what existed before either *angels* or *humans* were formed, which is not...

<summary>The author describes Divine Wisdom (the Virgin) as a pure, celestial being whose essence is incompatible with the physical world and who will be fully revealed during a future spiritual "Pentecost." The text uses alchemical and astrological symbolism—such as the sun, stars, and planets—to explain how this divine nature is birthed within the human spirit.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the personification of Divine Wisdom as a Virgin, moving from a description of her essential nature to her "organic faculties"—her spiritual senses—which allow her to interact with the divine realm while remaining untainted by the lower world.</meta>

...to be portrayed, until she comes to be essentially manifested within. Only her *organic faculties* <note>original: "Organical Faculties." Here referring to the spiritual equivalents of physical sensory organs.</note> are described to us, so that we may know what constitutes the Virgin's high descent <note>original: "Descent." Meaning her lineage or spiritual origin.</note> through these several *faculties*, as follows:

The *first faculty* set before me was that of *seeing*, seated in her eye, which appeared like a fiery ray and sparkled like a star, piercing all things in the heights and depths. It is an eye that does not slumber, but watches at the gate of every center <note>original: "Centers-Gate." In mystical theology, "centers" refer to the inner spiritual points or depths of the soul.</note> to spy on what goes in and out. This prevents any violation of her *virgin chastity*, ensuring her vow remains unbroken, by which she stands committed to her husband.

The *second faculty* is that of *hearing*, seated in the ear. It must be so *pure* <note>original: "Chaste."</note> that it turns away from all corrupt and distasteful language that does not express God’s immense love and goodness, or his greatness and sovereignty. The true Virgin's ear is turned inward to listen to the sweet voice of her bridegroom; she waits for the words from his lips, which are the strength and marrow of her life. She is in such union with the Deity that she cannot listen to any strange voice, for fear of being defiled by it. She feels the greatest danger and harm coming from "talking spirits" from the dark center of the soul; her virgin ear turns away from these and dares not remain within their astral counsel <note>original: "Astral counsel." Refers to worldly, psychic, or lower celestial influences as opposed to the pure divine word.</note>. Therefore, she turns her ear to those echoes, voices, and sounds that leave behind a sweetness, resisting any delight in corrupt or irrelevant conversations from the outside world. Thus, guarding both *eye* and *ear*, she continues to move forward in her heavenly sphere.

The *third organic faculty* is that of *smelling*. This may be understood in two ways: first, as the breathing in of *paradisiacal perfumes* <note>original: "Paradifical Perfumes." Scents originating from the divine realm of Paradise.</note> and those sweet, ravishing odors that are enough to renew a fading life and make it strongly active and vigorous. Secondly, the Virgin herself is a highly scented *odor*; her garments send out a fragrance far surpassing all the spicy mixtures of this earthly climate. How vile and despicable everything that has lost its heavenly nature <note>original: "Paradifical Property."</note> appears to those who occasionally breathe in these ravishing odors! If these scents could only last, they would never desire any other food. Shall I declare what has been made known to me regarding the—

What is the power of these *Virgin Perfumes*? It has been declared to me that they will recover a rotting soul, dissolve all the poison of Original Sin, restore dead flesh, and preserve it from rotting. It is a balsam <note>original: "Balsom." A healing, aromatic ointment or resin symbolizing spiritual restoration.</note> gifted with such a penetrating spirit that it can pass through stone and iron, flesh and bones. No one can know this until they have passed through the Virgin's womb <note>A mystical metaphor for spiritual rebirth or transformation within Divine Wisdom.</note>, and then they will confirm this testimony. Now it must be considered how intolerable all the foul plants of the Earth are to the Virgin, who constantly breathes this perfumed air. Whenever she makes a quick passage through this foul-smelling *Kedar* <note>original: "Kedar." A biblical reference to the "tents of Kedar" (Psalm 120:5), used here to symbolize the dark, unpleasant, and corrupted state of the world.</note>, she needs a strong-scented garland so that no infection can harm her virgin purity. To this end, this provision is made to keep out the evil scents that meet us everywhere in the walks of this world.

The *fourth faculty* is that of *tasting*. Here we find what it is that nourishes the Virgin; her food is within herself, and it is nothing other than the pure Godhead, which rises like a flowing spring from her own depths. She lives upon the spirit of the air, which maintains and fans the quintessential fire <note>original: "Quint-Essential Fire." In alchemy and mysticism, the "fifth essence" or the purest, highest form of fire/spirit.</note>. This is the first virgin-matter from which the soul was created, which in *Adam* became corrupted by the eating of the forbidden fruit. He could have lived upon the root of eternal life which he possessed within himself; he could have tasted the fruits of paradise <note>original: "Paradifical Fruits."</note> at his pleasure. Whatever was physical on the outside, he possessed in essence within the Virgin inside himself, so that he could choose whether or not to eat of them in a physical way. This has been a secret little understood until now; but God in Christ, through his manifestation, has revealed it. He has done so to let us know what we are to feed upon at the restoration of our virginity <note>In this mystical context, "virginity" refers to the soul's return to a state of original, uncorrupted purity and union with God.</note>, when the life’s nourishment will be within itself, without any dependence upon anything from the outside. Oh! A completely different experience will be known in the Virgin soul when she is wedded to her, even though she has the freedom to eat, just as *Adam* did before the fall. So here, the physical mouth may taste of these earthly fruits, but not out of necessity, because the life is centered in and sustained by the heart of the Godhead for the increase and growth of pure virgin nature. And truly, nothing but God can be the Virgin’s food; nothing is so well digested by her, for it is a flowing source of almighty strength and delightful tasting in infinite varieties. This be-

...[tree] being once found, the soul can no longer eat the fruits of the mixed tree <note>The Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil, which represents the duality of the fallen world.</note> in whose root the curse lies. That curse brings in death, which hides the Virgin from the soul's essence.

The *fifth faculty* is the *supersensory feeling*. What sense is more excellent than this? Can there be any greater confirmation than a living feeling, from which joy, peace, and assurance spring? The Virgin feels herself in the center of the Deity; she handles what does not appear to be tangible—namely, *Breath, Spirit, Fire,* and *Air*. In all of these, her feeling-motion <note>original: "feeling-motion." The intuitive, spiritual movement or interaction of the soul with divine elements.</note> exists in great serenity. A "center-life" <note>original: "Center-Life." A life rooted in the spiritual core or the presence of God.</note> that burns from the mirror of the Deity boils love up to such a height that nothing is felt but pleasure and ravishing delight. It is the heat and blood that runs through every vein of the Virgin-body, and it is an antidote against all fear and pain felt in the mortal senses. 

Oh, what deaths, aches, and griefs we have felt in our souls as well as in our bodies! Our minds have been in continual anxiety and trouble to see the state into which we have fallen. I speak of those who have been touched with the "fire-stone" <note>original: "Fire-Stone." Likely a reference to a purifying spiritual trial or the alchemical "philosopher's stone" that must precede spiritual healing.</note> of the Virgin, which must come before her healing medicine. And blessed are they who have first known the painful feeling under her severe discipline; for such people shall be her polished pillars on which she will engrave her Virgin name. This is as he says who is the true and faithful witness, who has come to take off the Virgin's veil so that we may see her in the mirror <note>original: "Glass." A looking-glass or mirror.</note> of perfection. Thus we may come to know and feel her, not as something distinct and separate from us, but as ourselves being entirely "virginized" <note>original: "Virginized." Transformed into a state of spiritual purity and divine wisdom.</note> in her essential deity. Then, the joyful feeling of the "in-breathing" of fire and air may be witnessed. 

We shall then see what will follow the harmony of these senses, which is the character of the Virgin-nature—the pattern by which we are to test ourselves. For this end, we are also called to look into her mirror, so that it may stir up a deep desire in us to have those senses awakened in us in their full excellence. 

Oh, great Goddess and Queen of all worlds! Will you, after so long a time of abandonment, appear once again? Who is it that has entreated your favor and gained a promise of a visit from you? Yet even a visit is too short, because nothing will satisfy us now unless you bestow yourself—with all your divine senses—as a "co-deified life" <note>original: "co-deified Life." A life lived in such union with God that it shares in the divine nature.</note>. Show us that you are now persuaded to join with the redeeming blood to restore your own...

Virginity, where you find humility and persistence in that personality which is beloved by the highest Wisdom and is the only spouse of God: know and remember us who are brought to your feet and have been under your severe discipline! Therefore, allow yourself to be our garland and crown!

Now we have advanced one step higher toward Wisdom’s heavenly throne, from which her golden scepter is stretched forth for us to grasp. This scepter passes through and beyond every cloud of the elements <note>original: "elementary Cloud." Clouds representing the physical, material world that obscure spiritual vision.</note> which the life of the senses has raised, so that we could not see our way to that sparkling Rock of the Deity. For this reason, the bright Sun now shines from her eternal orbit through the dark clouds, so that we might see every ascending step—twisting and winding so intricately that, without a guide sent expressly from that sphere, there is no possibility of ever reaching any of her gates which lead to the

<margin>These five gates were the five senses spoken of before. Now we proceed to the following gates.</margin>
*New Jerusalem.* These are shown to me to be twelve in number. Five of these were discussed in the previous chapter, and now we turn to her oracle <note>original: "Oracle." A source of divine wisdom or a place where such wisdom is revealed.</note> as our guide to reveal the other seven gates, along with the way that leads from one to the other. There, the hidden track of the Virgin’s Way may be found, treading upon that unknown ground beneath which a rich and precious stone is found, from which the highest blessing will abound.

The way to the first of these seven gates is very solitary and lonely. It is very rare to find even two people who agree to persist in it to the end, because it is so full of tearing thorns and frightening wild beasts. For here lies the plan of the horned beast <note>A symbolic representation of the devil or worldly power that opposes the spiritual journey.</note> to scatter and divide those who have agreed to travel in Wisdom's path. Also, the "Nimrod hunters" <note>original: "Nimrod Hunters." Referring to the biblical figure Nimrod, "a mighty hunter before the Lord"; here used to describe those in the world who aggressively pursue or persecute spiritual seekers.</note> of this world pursue them, seeking either to divide them or bring them back again. These are the oppositions we can expect on this path, against which we must be prepared and forearmed, so that our entrance through this gate is not prevented.

The inscription on this gate is *Abnegation or Renunciation of this Outward Principle.* <note>The "outward principle" refers to the material world and the fallen state of human existence.</note> The first question asked by the angel who guards this gate is: *“Have you come away as strangers and pilgrims? Will you no longer return to the commerce of Babylon?”* For the Prince of this city will only admit those who shall renounce all commerce...

Commerce with the Merchants of *Tarshish* <note>original: "Tarshish." A biblical city (often identified with Spain) famous for its fleet and immense maritime wealth; here it symbolizes the pursuit of worldly riches.</note>, though by this means all earthly riches are gained to keep up a worldly reputation and fame; but the Angel of this Gate will require good assurance that whoever comes up here is quite broken off from, and has no further dealing with, the Harlot daughter of *Sidon* <note>original: "Sidon." An ancient Phoenician city; in mystical literature, the "daughter of Sidon" often represents worldly commerce, vanity, or spiritual adultery.</note>, who has deceived the kingdoms of this world with her sorceries. Whoever they be that get through this strait gate <term>strait gate: A narrow, difficult, or restrictive passage; derived from Matthew 7:13–14.</term>, for them a sure and good foundation is laid to walk forward toward the second gate. But here another thing is to be considered before entrance can be given; for this Angel is commanded not to take anyone’s single security, but inquires whether Christ’s Spirit in them, and his Person for them, will stand engaged to perform all this agreement. For he dares not trust the soul upon a single promise—though it be fervent, unfeigned, and full of good will—it is still not sufficient if he is not able to maintain the ground of the Gate against his enemies, who will attempt to circumvent him. Therefore, we who have come to this point are to give the Name of our Security <note>Security: In a legal sense, a "security" is a person who takes responsibility for another's debt or behavior. Here, Christ acts as the guarantor for the pilgrim's soul.</note>, and so pass on freely to the second gate.

The second gate is called the wrestling or struggling gate; it is the Gate of Probation <term>probation: A period of testing or trial to prove one's character, faith, or fitness for a higher state.</term>, and before it stands a Center-Gulf. Here, things will arise that will command the pilgrim back into the worldly principle which he had sworn off and left behind. *Wisdom* stands by her Angel to call and invite the traveler to come through; but the gulf swells so very high that it appears impossible to get over. Legions of spirits follow here, crying out, *"What do you mean by taking such a great risk as to leave a place where there is a store of all good things for supply and maintenance?"* Thus temptations and assaults will beset the pilgrim, threatening hard and bitter things to him who is seeking his own native country and original virginity <note>original virginity: A term used in Christian mysticism to describe the soul’s restored state of purity and its original, unfallen union with the Divine.</note>, and who endeavors with great wrestling to get rid of all these gnawing vipers. *"What!"* they say, *"will you turn quite away from those pleasant joys in which others live, spending their days in God’s love and peace, and resting contented there, not attempting to pass this gulf until the limit of your life requires it?"* All this and much more will press in to turn the soul away from pursuing its Virgin Crown; and therefore this gate is called the Probation Gate. Now if the Spirit of Christ is so powerful in the soul as to disregard these temptations, being resolved to...

Oh! What a welcome feast do I see already prepared within this gate, to satisfy both the eye and the taste! The *Virgin* comes and greets the soul with an exchange of love, saying: "Come now and inherit true substance with me, you who have been on a weary journey to reach this gate of spiritual stability. <margin>By *Sophia* is meant *Virgin-Wisdom*.</margin>

### The Fourth Gate

The fourth gate is called the *Bountiful Gate*. From here, the richest gift is brought forth that has ever been received since *Adam* lost his Virgin <note>In certain mystical traditions, Adam was believed to have originally possessed a divine feminine counterpart or an internal "Virgin-Wisdom" which was lost at the Fall.</note>, who now here gives herself again to the soul. Oh, how great is this kindness! Who could have ever hoped for such degrees of advancement after so long a day of desertion and such a night of falling away <note>original: "Apostacy."</note>? We now hear an echo sounding that she will appear in her bounty. But to whom shall this gate stand open to receive this generosity from her hand? 

Is it not for those like *Ruth* <note>In the Bible, Ruth chose to leave her home in Moab to follow her mother-in-law and the God of Israel, eventually becoming an ancestor of King David.</note>, who has forsaken the *Moabite* land and come to be settled there, from where her eternal birth is derived? No sooner has she arrived there than she is adorned with all the Virgin’s ornaments, which are the various accomplishments in all heavenly powers and high abilities. These allow her to act in a divine, all-powerful manner—whether in a physical form or out of it, as shall be judged appropriate according to the place, time, or persons, and according to the region where the Bridegroom and his bride shall be, whether in the heights above or the depths beneath, where his and her rejoicing shall be found.

The fourth gate is called the *Bountiful Gate*. From here, the richest gift is brought forth that has ever been received since *Adam* lost his Virgin <note>original: "lost his Virgin." Oh, how great is this kindness! Who could have ever hoped for such levels of advancement after such a long day of abandonment and a night of falling away <note>original: "Apostacy."</note>? We now hear a resounding echo that she will appear in her bounty. 

But for whom shall this gate stand open to receive this generosity from her hand? Is it not for *Ruth* <note>Referring to the biblical Ruth, who left her own people to follow Naomi, serving as a symbol of the soul's devotion to God.</note>, who has forsaken the *Moabite* land and come to settle where her eternal birth <note>original: "Nativity."</note> originates? No sooner has she arrived there than she is adorned with all the Virgin's ornaments, which are the various accomplishments in all heavenly <term>original: "supernal." These allow her to act in a divine, all-powerful manner, whether in a physical <term>original: "corporeal."</term> form or out of it, as shall be judged appropriate regarding place, time, or people. This follows the region where the Bridegroom and his Bride shall be, whether in the heights above or the depths beneath, where his and her rejoicing shall be

...in each other, through the breaking open of the Center, from which the *Virgin-Tincture* <note>original: "Virgin-Tincture." This is the gift that the Virgin will bestow upon the steadfast <note>original: "fixed."</note> soul, for a greater manifestation of God’s many wonders throughout the habitable part of this Earth. ### The Fifth Gate

The fifth gate bears the title of *Wisdom’s Factory of everything managed by her Magia* <note>original: "Magia." A term used in Christian mysticism to describe divine or spiritual power through which God acts upon the world, distinct from common magic.</note> *from her own stock and principle.* This is very different from all the merchandise used in this visible world, which is completely shut out of her gates. Therefore, *Wisdom* will take no one to be her agents <note>original: "Factors." In the 17th century, a "factor" was a commercial agent or trader who managed business for another.</note> except those who have renounced all *Babylonian* trade <note>original: "Babylonish." Referring to the corrupt, greedy systems of the world.</note>, which is maintained by bearing the mark of the beast through the cleverness of human reason <note>original: "introduced subtilty of Reason."</note>.

A new and wonderful way is prescribed for those who shall have the mark and name of the *Virgin’s white stone* <note>Reference to Revelation 2:17: "To him that overcometh... I will give him a white stone, and in the stone a new name written."</note>. By this, they shall be empowered to conduct a free and mighty trade within this heavenly city. This trade will make all of *Babylon’s* wares—both spiritual goods and physical sustenance—unwanted and obsolete <note>original: "grow out of request."</note>. This will happen as soon as some "virgin-passengers" <note>Those who have purified themselves spiritually to enter Wisdom's realm.</note> have entered this gate. This occurs as the person of *Christ* and his *Virgin* transmit themselves as a spiritual root, from whence all the fruitful powers of the Holy *Spirit* may be proven. For although the miracles of former days were great and excellent—such as those performed by our Lord, the prophets before him, and the apostles after him, including the healing of the *sick*, the raising of the *dead*, the restoring of the *lame*, the making of the *mute* <note>original: "dumb"</note> to *speak*, the *deaf* to *hear*, and the *blind* to *see* (all of which were performed by our Lord both inwardly upon the soul and outwardly upon the body)—yet the Lord has given full assurance that not only will all these be revived, but that the *Magical* center <note>original: "Magical Center"; in this mystical tradition, it refers to the spiritual source of divine manifestation and power</note> shall open with a greater variety than ever before. 

Thus, the greatest wonders are still to be manifested. We are appointed to wait for them until this Projecting Gate opens for us, where all miracles will be brought forth anew for the crown and glory of the Virgin Bride and her followers. *May the Lord Jesus hasten to open this gate for those who are under his strong and powerful impulses <note>original: "Drivings"</note>!*

From that state, the soul may most easily ascend and fly to be an eye within the *Globe-Eye*, before which everything stands naked and open—whatever has come forth from the center or ground of God’s Globe of Eternity. Therefore, those who find such favor as to be turned inward and surrounded by this *Eye* may well come to understand the foundations of divine *Magic* <note>original: "Magia"; a term often used in mystical texts to distinguish spiritual, God-given power from common sorcery or trickery.</note>. Through this, great upheavals and mighty changes may be brought about in this world’s region, where God will have many occasions for divine *Magic* to act on His behalf. ...magicians are in their first paradisiacal callings, which the Holy Spirit has received a commission to place some holy, qualified souls into once again, so that they may be leading examples <note>original: "Presidents"</note> for others.

<margin>
I think I hear some say, "This is such a new model that it is wonderfully strange; please open the mystery of it even more fully."
</margin>

But it may be further asked, *What is this Magia? And how can it be practiced to change the entire state of things, both internally and externally?*

<margin>
Answer: I can only give out what the measuring line of the Spirit has fathomed, and no more.
</margin>

It is explained to us this way: It is an arising infinity <note>original: "Infinitum"</note> that forms itself into a birth. Its substance can be described in no other way than as the unknown being of God himself, who is a pure, virtual, working power within a finite being. From this, a flowing source of generating powers acts outward from what lies hidden so inconceivably that it cannot be seen, felt, or heard outside of itself—except as it produces great and marvelous effects upon whatever it moves. This is what is called the *Magia* <note>Divine magic or the spiritual power of God working through a person</note> of the Holy Spirit, defined as he has pleased to reveal it to us.

Now it remains to ask, *By whom, and how, is this to be practiced? And for what purpose is it to be manifested to such a high degree?*

<margin>
Magia is the faith in the operation of the Holy Spirit.
</margin>

Regarding the first question—*By whom is this to be exerted or practiced?*—much has been said already. We will only mention one thing, which is the primary qualification of those to whom this honor will be given: namely <note>original: "viz."</note>, they must make a firm and fixed resolution to follow only this magical calling. They must reject all other callings that proceed from the *astral birth* <note>The influences of the physical world or the stars upon the human soul, as opposed to divine influence</note>, which pour in like floods to drown the little spark of faith from which the *Magia* tree must first grow and appear in all its various fruits. 

This, I say, is what must be done on our part if we wish to be adepts <note>original: "Adeptists"; those who have mastered a spiritual or mystical art</note> in the supersensual <note>Beyond the reach of the physical senses</note> working wheel, which will lead us back into our lost dominion, to be in God once again. But then watchfulness is required to repel and judge every whirling motion and thought that rises to thwart the way of *Magia*. This way is actually nothing else but a most pure, single act of faith, heightened to such a degree that it becomes a kind of omnipotence—if the soul can only get free from the "life of sense" <note>A life governed by physical perceptions and worldly reasoning rather than spiritual reality</note>. This sensory life is the soul's great and home-grown enemy, which will hardly allow the soul to make its escape, but follows closely at its heels until the hand of faith grows strong enough to use the magical...

<summary>The author defines "Magia" as the infinite, creative power of the Holy Spirit working through a person and explains that achieving this state requires a total rejection of worldly influences in favor of an intense, omnipotent faith.</summary>
<keywords>Magia, Holy Spirit, Faith, Astral Birth, Adepts, Paradisiacal Calling, Life of Sense</keywords>

--- Page 153 ---
<meta>This page continues the discussion on the discipline required for those entering the "Magia" of the Holy Spirit, focusing on the need for a spiritual "second birth" to overcome the limitations of the senses.</meta>

(65)

...rod, which may break their binding circle. In this way, we may gain a swift passage into boundless liberty and become experts in **theosophical science** <note>Divine wisdom or the knowledge of God's inner workings</note>. This science is not from man, nor does it follow any human rational wisdom. Instead, it is like the pure, fiery breath of the Holy Spirit merging with the fire-essence of the soul to drive it upward into a body capable of all **magical** operation. 

To freely activate this life, we are warned to stop all other ways and occupations and fix our minds only on this. We must close our ears to the pleas and cries of the senses, which would keep us nursing at the breast of the creature <note>The physical, created world</note>, even though nothing but death, sorrow, and the curse can be drawn from it. It is indeed natural to act this way as long as we remain in our first birth <note>Our initial, fallen, and physical state of existence</note>. It will be difficult to break free from this until the other—the second birth—begins to rise in the soul's center. 

Within this second birth, there is a powerful force capable of overcoming and breaking the yoke that has so often nearly strangled this **magical birth** of life, preventing it from breathing freely. Therefore, let us now take care to flee from our mortal senses and exclude them entirely from that newly established throne, council, and court. There, in pure focus <note>original: "Abstraction"</note> and a childlike spiritual posture, we may wait to learn how to act according to our individual gifts in this high and supernatural **magia** <note>Spiritual power or divine action</note>. 

In doing so, we may demonstrate the lineage and birth from which we come—created after God’s own likeness in power, wisdom, and purity. This glorifies Him in a new **tabernacle-body**, **magically** raised. Once this is completed within the outward form of our visible physical body <note>original: "Corporeity"</note>, an unchangeable **priesthood** begins within the most holy place. This priesthood is not carried out like the symbolic and fleshly **administration** <note>The Old Testament systems of law and sacrifice</note> that required atonements and sin offerings. In this greater and more perfect **tabernacle**, there will be no need for such things because sin has ended. The Holy Spirit has obtained a more excellent **ministration** <note>Service or work</note> within us: specifically, **offering the first fruits of redemption**, a level that no previous spiritual era ever reached.

But here it may be asked, *What kind of offerings will these first fruits of redemption be?*

This is a question that cannot be fully answered until we are completely perfected in this **tabernacle**, which so far is only

<meta>catchword: only</meta>

<summary>The author describes a spiritual transformation where the soul moves beyond physical senses through a "second birth," eventually inhabiting a spiritual "tabernacle-body." This state enables a new form of divine priesthood and the offering of the "first fruits of redemption" beyond traditional religious rituals.</summary>

<keywords>Theosophical Science, second birth, Magia, Tabernacle-Body, Holy Spirit, redemption, spiritual priesthood, physical senses</keywords>

--- Page 154 ---
<meta>This page continues the discussion on the "magia"—the divine power of the soul—and how it allows the believer to offer spiritual sacrifices that can transform both the inner person and the physical world. The previous page emphasized the need for a "second birth" to access this power.</meta>

(66)

...only a raising up. But whatever of these **immunities** <note>original: "Immunities"; here meaning spiritual privileges or exemptions from the laws of the fallen world</note> has been expressed by the Great Priest and revealed to me from the **Tabernacle Testimony** <note>The revelation or witness proceeding from God’s holy dwelling</note> shall be shared for the sake of those who may have the right to bring such perfect offerings into the most holy and separated place. This occurs in the day of their **restoration** together with us, as we expect the fulfillment of all divine **ordinances** and **consecrations** for the perfecting of everyone who comes here.

But now, to define what kind of **offerings** we shall bring as priests to God: they are the fruits of the **magia** <note>Divine spiritual power or the creative action of the soul</note> in its outward-flowing operation. This actually sends forth a most powerful spirit from its own essential spring of Spirit, of which God is the only pure origin. What is sent forth but the effect of a new generation of heavens and earth? 

The **magia** works in this manner, saying, "*Let the old tabernacle, with all its shadowy appearances, pass away and be no more*." And it is so; for the force of the outgoing Spirit joins with the **Word** and makes it substantial, whether it is expressed vocally or not. The centered decree passes into action. Although the Spirit carries all power with it—either to nullify and make void, or to provide a new plantation and a heavenly, splendid formation upon creatures and things—it works for a lustrous **transformation** of what is fading and dying, so that **immortality** may be renewed, whether in rational beings, animals, or plants. 

The matter of the **one element** <note>In theosophy, the pure, spiritual substance that underlies all holy or "paradisiacal" forms</note> is so immersed and condensed in a **magical spirit** that it is able to give life even where the very **face of death** appears—whether someone is dead in a spiritual sense, according to the saying, "*And you he has quickened, who were dead in trespasses and sins*" <note>A reference to Ephesians 2:1; "quickened" means "made alive"</note>. 

Who is this, in the person of **Christ**, but the same **Holy Ghost** in his outgoing power? Through our persons, he influences and gives a **resurrection** to a dead life, whether in ourselves or others, as the need requires. Then, he also acts upon the very mortal life to prolong it and to secure it from pain and sickness—yes, even from death itself. 

But this quickening Spirit is only given to those who have died and suffered with **Christ** in the **flesh**, and have thereby overcome the **world**. Therefore, let no one think they can reach this degree of power, might, and dignity until they are **born again from the dead**. This means a cessation of the earthly life sparked by the stars <note>In the philosophy of Jacob Boehme, the "stars" represent the cosmic and astrological forces that govern the fallen, physical world</note>, which generate...

<meta>catchword: earthly</meta>

<summary>This page describes the "magia" as a divine power that allows spiritual priests to transform the physical world and renew immortality. This power, which can even overcome sickness and death, is only accessible to those who have undergone a spiritual resurrection through Christ.</summary>

<keywords>magia, Holy Spirit, priesthood, transformation, immortality, quickening, second birth, Tabernacle Testimony, healing</keywords>
