<meta>This page continues the discussion of the "magia"—the divine power of the soul—explaining that one must move away from the "sensuality" of the earthly life to access the spiritual "tabernacle-body." It emphasizes the transition from a servant of sin to a "king in God's house" through the power of the Holy Spirit.</meta>

...earthly motions and thoughts, which work through sensuality, from which there must be a departure in reference to those great things that are to follow. I have often been brought to see the limit of the *first-born life*, which exists between *time* and *eternity* in the active essences of sin. Through this, I have become so wisely knowledgeable that I do not grieve or feel any reluctance regarding the sentence of a total ending <note>original: "Expiration"</note> of such a harmful life, which keeps me out of God’s **tabernacle-body** <note>The spiritual body or dwelling place of the soul in union with God</note>, where I can no longer die. 

Therefore, the Holy Spirit, out of the **virgin body** <note>original: "Virgin-Body"; a state of spiritual purity or the refined spiritual form of the soul</note>, cries and calls to those who would become entirely *magical* <note>original: "Magical"; referring here to the exercise of divine, supernatural power</note> to offer up their first-born rational life as a sacrifice. Then, in truth, we shall bring a different kind of fruits and offerings; we will no longer be subjects or servants to *sin, the beast, or the dragon* <note>References to the fallen nature and satanic influences mentioned in the Book of Revelation</note>, but will be freed through the rising birth. This birth will mount and fly over all those things that kept the old life under great fear and bondage. In this state, we shall become kings in God’s house and members of his own family. 

Oh! What has been let down here by *golden drops* from the *head-fountain*! O God, let it all mingle powerfully for life-contact <note>original: "contaction"; the spark or joining of divine life</note> with the more noble part within us. May this great and perfect tabernacle be joined together <note>original: "compacted"</note> indissolubly in its own secret way, so that we may dwell securely. There, let there be nothing of *violence, oppression, or fear*, but instead let us receive power from the rising body of the Holy Spirit to suppress and expel whatever is against us. This is what the Lord has declared by a significant word, and he will see it fulfilled in anyone who possesses such a spirit of resolution to go forward in this great enterprise—to encounter and rush through every crowd that would stop or push aside those who are ready to venture <note>original: "venter"</note> their all for it.

Now, something may be said regarding those high purposes for which God is resolved to raise up his principal *magia* <note>Divine spiritual power or creative action</note>.

*First*, to redress, comfort, and relieve the groaning creation, which is in spiritual labor—even those who are in *famine and death*, in *agonies, sorrow, and heaviness* through various temptations and the buffetings and wiles of Satan. To these, the *magical balsam* <note>A spiritual healing ointment or remedy derived from divine power</note> shall be given for a cure by those who have first risen in the tabernacle-body of God. They shall bind all *evil*

<summary>This page describes the necessity of sacrificing the "rational life" to attain a divine, "magical" state within God's family. It also introduces the purpose of this divine power: to heal the suffering creation with a "magical balsam."</summary>

...spirits, and an effective power <note>original: "virtual Power"; meaning power that is inherently effective or potent in its essence</note> shall go forth from them to act as if Christ himself were there in person.

And in the second place, this power shall extend itself to the removal of all outward illnesses <note>original: "Maladies"</note> of the body, all suffering related to extreme poverty <note>original: "Penury"</note> and want, and all weariness, hard labor, and anxious worry for the needs and requirements of the body.

A further addition to the previous subject: Know then, that in a miraculous way, this will be made like healing waters flowing from the threshold of the sanctuary. These waters shall restore the dry and parched Earth, making it bud and flourish as it did before the Curse <note>A reference to the biblical "Curse of the Ground" following the Fall of Man in Genesis</note> overwhelmed it. Understand this both inwardly regarding spiritual fruitfulness, and outwardly regarding the physical world <note>original: "Elementary state"</note>. 

Great also will be the trust granted by the mighty **Jehovah** <note>original: "Jehovah"; a name for God in the Hebrew Bible</note> for the management of his "little flock." This trust will be put into the hands of some who are to be set apart as truly good and primary shepherds. These are shepherds who can both give up and lay down their life for the chosen sheep, who are to be gathered in—not by sounds or words, but by a supernatural infusion <note>original: "Magical Impregnation"; the author uses "magical" to describe the direct, miraculous operation of divine power upon the soul</note> which can send in a breath of life to quicken what is as if dead. 

This is what we now daily expect from the rich spiritual provisions <note>original: "Furniture"; the inward qualities or divine treasures belonging to the spiritual state</note> which will come down with the **tabernacle-body**. Oh come, holy souls! Let us be in a watchful posture; for I see in the Spirit that the morning of this day begins to clear quickly. The bright clouds are opening, the face of our heavens appear, and the separating veil is all tearing away. We may now pass out of the first tabernacle and stay permanently within the most holy place. There, we shall have no cause to fear that God will move away from now on, because we ourselves shall be brought up to that height of purity where all offerings shall be most pleasant and powerful, coming from the everlasting flame of the holy altar. 

From that altar, ascending powers will go up to bring down such gifts as will enrich everything more abundantly than what came down on the day of **Pentecost** <note>The day the Holy Spirit descended upon the Apostles, as recorded in the Book of Acts</note>. Those gifts were only the first fruits and lasted only a little while; but here is such a shower of eternal powers falling down upon this second call to the **Jerusalem** waiters <note>Those waiting for the manifestation of the "New Jerusalem" or God's kingdom on earth</note> as shall bring forth the harvest of the Lord’s joy. His Spirit works for the ripening of these fruits...

most powerful. What is left for us to do? Only to drink in the falling dews and dropping showers, and to grow like lilies that know neither heat nor drought <note>original: "Draught"</note>, nor toil nor care.

Here follows an account of a deeply ecstatic <note>original: "ravishing"</note> communion enjoyed with the Trinity. However, do not view this merely as an emotional high <note>original: "Elivation"</note>, but as coming from a spirit transported as if into another region of light—half of which cannot be expressed in words.

The throne of the Most Holy is now set <note>original: "pitched"</note> in the center of the **Heart of Jesus**, joined together with my own heart. From there, such streams of life flow that they renew a perpetual **spring of joy**. For the Lord declared the fullness of every blessing from the circular throne of his presence, showering <note>original: "bedewing"</note> me with the **water of life** from the **glassy sea** <note>A reference to the "sea of glass" mentioned in Revelation 4:6</note>, mingled with the **fire of love**. He assured me that the Curse, which is attached to fallen nature, should now flee away from God and the **Lamb’s throne**. The Lord has chosen me to be this throne for him, in which the scepter of his kingdom shall rule over everything through divine omnipotence. 

Upon hearing this thunder-cry, which passed through my heavens, all **nations, tongues, languages, spirits, and powers** surrendered to him. He has come to establish his throne and create all things new, confirming his covenant in the newness of the Spirit. He comes to nullify everything belonging to the dead and fruitless life that relies on the literal law <note>original: "Letter-Precept"; referring to a rigid, external following of religious rules without spiritual life</note>, which could never destroy the root of sin. 

The Word of Truth now makes it known that nothing else will ever remove the **Serpent’s sting** <note>A reference to the effects of sin and the Fall of Man</note> except the overflowing of the burning oil from the **Sea of Glass**, which is released <note>original: "broken up"</note> by the powerful movement <note>original: "violent stroke"</note> of the **Holy Ghost**. All **sanctifications** and **renewals** are imperfect and subject to change until this comes to rise as a covering, making the physical <note>original: "gross"</note> body transparent. We must observe, then, that whenever God truly comes to enthrone himself in anyone, this burning sea will be felt. From this sea, the fiery...

<meta>This page continues the description of the believer's internal spiritual transformation, where the external nations and languages mentioned previously are now being subjected to the internal rule of Christ's throne within the soul.</meta>

(70)

Lamps go forth, which guard the throne. Oh! It is the great and high achievement that a spirit veiled in a physical body <note>original: "vail'd with Corporeity"</note> can hope for: to have God descend in his sovereign throne-authority <note>original: "Throne-dominion"</note> and cast down all other thrones where dark, fiery spirits have played their part within the inner sanctuary <note>original: "Tent"</note> of the mind. All of these are now destroyed and consumed by the pure, flaming throne, which provides sufficient evidence that God's kingdom has now moved beyond mere words and has emerged as a felt power. 

Oh, the joys and pleasures my soul feels in its first rising! This is infinitely more precious than anything previously described, even by Christ’s own person <note>original: "Personality"</note>, whose appearance portrayed the *glory of his kingdom to me* in so many ways. But for him to come now and establish it within me will, in due time, produce things different from what has been believed or expected by those who hesitate and fear. They have cried out, "The Lord is delaying his coming," while scolding and mistreating <note>original: "beating"</note> their fellow servants and demanding signs and wonders, without which they can believe nothing. 

But let all such dismissive spirits know that the throne and kingdom of the Lamb may be formed, fashioned, furnished, and enriched with glory over a long period of time. All of this is carried out very secretly until the entire mystery of the kingdom is first completed internally, before it emerges to make a visible display through an awe-inspiring <note>original: "terrible," meaning inspiring dread or wonder</note> performance of wonders. 

Know also that those who have come to feel the kingdom in its invisible powers and the seven lamps of fire <note>A reference to the seven lamps of fire before the throne of God in Revelation 4:5</note> burning—those who sit with Christ upon the throne, reigning over and judging all the "nations" within themselves—shall be given judgment and power over all those who slandered and belittled <note>original: "set light by"</note> them during the time of those others' outward rule and authority, while the suffering lambs of God were still immature <note>original: "in their Minority"</note>. 

But we will not worry about judgment being given or authority being exercised from the throne of the Lamb within ourselves just yet; instead, we must continue until it is fully perfected and the kingdom is established. Our current task is to watch and ensure that every lamp is fed from the sea of glass <note>original: "glassy Sea"</note>, so that as they burn with light and the flames of love, a crystalline throne may be maintained for the entire Godhead <note>original: "Deity"</note>. Truly, while such a thing as this is felt and witnessed, there is a most enchanting <note>original: "ravishing"</note> tranquility. Oh, what could be greater than to have Christ seated upon his throne

<summary>The author describes the internal establishment of God's throne within the mind, which consumes dark spirits and brings a felt power beyond mere words. She argues that this spiritual kingdom is built secretly within the believer before it ever manifests as outward signs or wonders.</summary>
